_source line 1063 · 2026-05-24T17:02:33.750Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01YcSRSsoFyoXAdk7NncMCVN`</summary>

```text
1	\begin{abstract}
2	LLM-based Document-level Relation Extraction (DocRE) now matches or exceeds encoder baselines in extraction accuracy, yet these methods output bare relation triplets without identifying supporting sentences. Encoder-based evidence methods (SAIS, DREEAM) provide supporting sentences but trail LLM approaches in accuracy, creating a gap between extraction performance and output verifiability.
3	We formalize evidence-grounded DocRE as structured generation and compare GRPO, DPO, and RSFT across three fully evaluated scales (1.7B, 4B, 8B) with 14B validation, two model families, and two datasets. The evidence tax---extraction cost of generating evidence---is scale-dependent: beneficial at 1.7B ($-$5.63pp dev Ign-F1) but approximately neutral at $\geq$4B (+0.25$\pm$0.47pp at 4B; positive $=$ cost). Gradient-based optimization amplifies evidence-extraction competition (GRPO catastrophic, DPO gradual), while RSFT with NLI-based scoring avoids decoupling entirely through output filtering rather than gradient pressure.
4	RSFT consistently reduces training seed variance (complete pairwise seed separation on LLaMA, Cohen's $d{=}2.58$; non-overlapping $\chi^2$ CIs at 1.7B). Our 1.7B RSFT model (51.19 Ign-F1, std 0.09pp) approaches 8B SFT performance (50.19, std 3.23pp) at 4.7$\times$ fewer parameters. Test-set evaluation preserves model ranking across all evaluated scales (Spearman $\rho{=}1.0$).
5	\end{abstract}
6	
```

````text
1	---
2	description: 用 PaperBanana 生成高品质学术概念图（架构/流程/framework）。统计图不要用 PaperBanana，用 draw-paper-figure。需要 GOOGLE_API_KEY。
3	name: paper-banana
4	scope: global
5	tags:
6	  - visualization
7	  - paper
8	  - matplotlib
9	  - llm-codegen
10	  - conceptual-diagram
11	version: 4
12	tools:
13	  - request_secret
14	  - poll_secret_request
15	---
16	
17	# Paper Banana — 高品质学术概念图生成
18	
19	PaperBanana 由 Gemini Pro 自动写 matplotlib 代码，VLM Critic 评估渲染结果并迭代优化，输出顶会级别 300 DPI 概念图。
20	
21	**质量底线：每张图必须达到顶会论文的美观度——高级感、专业感、精致感。绝不接受 matplotlib 默认样式的廉价感。**
22	
23	## 适用范围
24	
25	| 场景 | 用什么 |
26	|------|--------|
27	| **概念图**（架构图、流程图、framework 示意） | **PaperBanana** ← 本 skill |
28	| **统计图**（scatter、bar、line、heatmap、forest） | **draw-paper-figure.skill.md**。**不要用 PaperBanana 画统计图** |
29	| 离线 / 无 GOOGLE_API_KEY | **draw-paper-figure.skill.md** |
30	
31	## 执行环境
32	
33	**PaperBanana 在本机（EC2）运行，不要 SSH 到远程服务器。**
34	
35	EC2（ap-northeast-1 东京）可直接访问 Gemini API，无需代理。
36	
37	## 前置条件
38	
39	### Step 1：获取 GOOGLE_API_KEY
40	
41	```python
42	request_secret(key_id="google_api_key", project="&lt;project_name>", purpose="PaperBanana 概念图生成")
43	# 等待 Director 审批后
44	poll_secret_request(request_id="<返回的 request_id>", project="&lt;project_name>")
45	```
46	
47	如果密钥尚未存储，告知 Director 需要 GOOGLE_API_KEY。
48	
49	### Step 2：安装验证（每次使用前必须检查）
50	
51	```bash
52	# 检查是否已安装
53	paperbanana --version 2>/dev/null || {
54	  echo "PaperBanana 未安装，正在安装..."
55	  pip install paperbanana -q
56	  paperbanana --version
57	}
58	```
59	
60	如果 `paperbanana --version` 失败（命令不存在），必须先 `pip install paperbanana -q` 安装。版本需 ≥ 0.1.2。
61	
62	**设计前必读** `skills/global/paper-visualization.skill.md` 的概念图 L1（防丑 7 条）+ L2（叙事 6 条）+ L3 Pattern + Checklist。统计图部分不适用于 PaperBanana。
63	
64	## 命令：generate
65	
66	概念图用 `generate` 子命令。**必须显式指定 `--vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro`**（默认模型 `gemini-2.0-flash` 已废弃，不指定会崩溃）。**`--iterations 5`**。
67	
68	## 完整流程
69	
70	### Step 1：写意图描述文件
71	
72	将详细设计描述写入文件（太长不适合命令行参数）：
73	
74	```bash
75	cat > /tmp/fig_intent.txt << 'INTENT'
76	Three-panel academic figure for a top-venue paper (full width, aspect ratio ~3.2:1, figsize 14x4.5 inches):
77	
78	Panel (a) 'The Phenomenon' (left, ~38% width):
79	Grouped bar chart. TF bars: dark navy (#2C3E50). AR bars: academic red (#C0392B).
80	Exact data: Base TF=0.501 AR=0.019, ThreadGate TF=0.504 AR=0.064, ...
81	Y-axis 'Mid-turn GAR' range 0–0.55. Only left+bottom spines. Serif font 10pt.
82	Add bracket annotation '8-17x gap' between bar groups.
83	
84	Panel (b) 'Diagnostic Chain' (center, ~24% width):
85	Vertical flowchart, 4 rounded boxes connected by thin gray arrows:
86	  Step 1: light gray (#F5F6F7) box, thin border (#DEE2E6), 'Gate Analysis' + subtitle. Green ✓
87	  Step 2: same style, 'PCGrad Exclusion'. Red ✗
88	  Step 3: same style, 'Representation Check'. Red ✗
89	  Step 4: light green (#D4EDDA) background, 'Conclusion: Exposure bias'. Green ✓
90	No axes, no spines. Clean infographic style.
91	
92	Panel (c) 'Constructive Evidence' (right, ~38% width):
93	Line plot. Data points: (0, 0.064), (3, 0.556), (5, 0.727).
94	Navy line (#2C3E50), open circles (white fill, navy edge, width 2).
95	Light blue fill (#EBF5FB, alpha 0.4) under curve.
96	Annotate '11x recovery' in muted gray, 9pt italic.
97	
98	OVERALL STYLE:
99	- 300 DPI, white background, publication quality
100	- Serif font (Times New Roman or similar) throughout
101	- Panel labels '(a)', '(b)', '(c)' in 11pt bold at top-left
102	- Generous white space (wspace=0.35)
103	- Muted academic palette: navy #2C3E50, red #C0392B, green #27AE60, gray #7F8C8D
104	- NO matplotlib default styling: no gray background, no thick gridlines, no tab10 colors
105	- Drop shadows on boxes: subtle, 2px offset, 10% opacity
106	- Rounded corners 8px on all rectangles
107	- Arrow heads: small, elegant, not bulky
108	INTENT
109	```
110	
111	### Step 2：运行 generate
112	
113	```bash
114	GOOGLE_API_KEY="[REDACTED]" paperbanana generate \
115	  --input /tmp/fig_intent.txt \
116	  --caption "Three-panel diagnostic framework: (a) TF vs AR performance gap, (b) systematic diagnosis chain, (c) beta-steering recovery evidence" \
117	  --vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro \
118	  --output figures/paper/fig1_overview.png \
119	  --iterations 5
120	```
121	
122	### Step 3：视觉检查（不可跳过）
123	
124	**Read 生成的 PNG 文件**，视觉评估以下维度：
125	
126	1. **高级感**：是否有顶会论文的精致感？还是看起来像课程作业？
127	2. **颜色**：是否有语义（不是随机分配）？是否和谐、不刺眼？
128	3. **文字**：缩到 column width 后是否 ≥ 7pt 可读？
129	4. **布局**：留白是否合理？元素是否对齐？
130	5. **细节**：圆角、阴影、箭头是否精致？边框是否统一？
131	
132	**不满意 → 必须修改意图描述文件，重新生成。**
133	
134	### Step 4：转 PDF
135	
136	```bash
137	python3 -c "
138	import matplotlib; matplotlib.use('Agg')
139	import matplotlib.pyplot as plt, matplotlib.image as mpimg
140	img = mpimg.imread('figures/paper/fig1_overview.png')
141	h, w = img.shape[:2]
142	fig = plt.figure(figsize=(w/300, h/300), dpi=300)
143	ax = fig.add_axes([0,0,1,1]); ax.imshow(img); ax.axis('off')
144	plt.savefig('figures/paper/fig1_overview.pdf', bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0)
145	plt.close()
146	"
147	```
148	
149	## 意图描述质量红线
150	
151	Intent 质量直接决定图的质量。必须包含以下 7 项（缺一不可）：
152	
153	1. **空间布局**：每个 panel 的宽度占比（如 38%/24%/38%）
154	2. **组件形状 + 尺寸**：圆角半径 8px、figsize (14, 4.5)
155	3. **颜色 hex 码**：不说"蓝色"，说 "#2C3E50"，绑定语义
156	4. **所有文字内容**：box label、副标题、箭头 annotation
157	5. **尺寸规格**：DPI 300、字号 10-12pt body / 8-9pt labels
158	6. **细节规格**：阴影偏移 2px / 10% opacity、箭头粗细、边框宽度
159	7. **反面约束**："NO matplotlib default styling, NO gray background, NO tab10 colors"
160	
161	### 美学风格关键词（必须在 intent 中出现）
162	
163	```
164	STYLE REQUIREMENTS:
165	- Publication quality for a TOP venue (NeurIPS/ICML/EMNLP)
166	- Elegant, premium, sophisticated — NOT cheap or homework-like
167	- Subtle drop shadows (2px, 10% opacity) on boxes
168	- Rounded corners (8px) on all rectangles
169	- Thin, elegant arrow heads — not bulky matplotlib defaults
170	- Serif font (Times) for labels, sans-serif (Helvetica) for annotations
171	- Muted, harmonious color palette — NO saturated primary colors
172	- Generous white space between elements
173	- Consistent visual weight across all panels
174	```
175	
176	### 反面教材（这样的 intent 一定画不好）
177	
178	- "Draw a pipeline diagram with three stages"
179	- "Architecture diagram for our system"
180	
181	太模糊。LLM 会用 matplotlib 默认样式填充所有你没指定的细节，结果就是廉价感。
182	
183	## 迭代策略（至少 3 轮外部迭代，Hero Figure 至少 5 轮）
184	
185	PaperBanana 内部有 VLM Critic 迭代（`--iterations 5`），但这还不够。你必须做**外部迭代**：
186	
187	### 第 1 轮：基础生成
188	运行 PaperBanana → Read PNG → 识别大方向问题（布局、配色、结构）
189	
190	### 第 2 轮：结构修复
191	修改意图描述（布局占比、颜色 hex、组件关系）→ 重新运行 → Read PNG
192	
193	### 第 3 轮：细节打磨
194	修改意图描述（字号、阴影、对齐、箭头粗细）→ 重新运行 → Read PNG
195	
196	### 第 4 轮（Hero Figure）：风格统一
197	线宽 / 字号 / 间距全图统一？与论文其他图配色一致？→ 重新运行 → Read PNG
198	
199	### 第 5 轮（Hero Figure）：最终 polish
200	像素级检查 → 缩到 column width 后文字 ≥ 7pt？→ 终极判断：放在 NeurIPS best paper 里违和吗？
201	
202	### 判断标准
203	
204	**可以交付**：看起来像 NeurIPS 2025 best paper 里的图。
205	**不可交付**：看起来像 matplotlib gallery 的示例图。
206	
207	## 失败回退
208	
209	如果 PaperBanana 多次崩溃（API 错误、模型不兼容）：
210	
211	1. 确认 `--vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro` 是否指定
212	2. 确认 GOOGLE_API_KEY 是否有效：`curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?key=$GOOGLE_API_KEY"` 应返回 200
213	3. 如果仍然失败，回退 matplotlib 手写：
214	   - Read `skills/global/paper-visualization.skill.md` 获取 L1-L4 设计标准
215	   - 写 Python 脚本手动画概念图（FancyBboxPatch、FancyArrowPatch 等）
216	   - **同样必须 Read PNG 做视觉检查，至少迭代 3 轮**
217	   - **同样必须达到顶会级别的美观度**
218	   - 保存 .py 脚本到 figures/paper/ 目录便于后续修改
219	
````

```text
Let me count the words in the abstract. I need to count just the text content, not LaTeX commands.

The abstract text:
"LLM-based Document-level Relation Extraction (DocRE) now matches or exceeds encoder baselines in extraction accuracy, yet these methods output bare relation triplets without identifying supporting sentences. Encoder-based evidence methods (SAIS, DREEAM) provide supporting sentences but trail LLM approaches in accuracy, creating a gap between extraction performance and output verifiability.
We formalize evidence-grounded DocRE as structured generation and compare GRPO, DPO, and RSFT across three fully evaluated scales (1.7B, 4B, 8B) with 14B validation, two model families, and two datasets. 

The evidence tax—the extraction cost of generating evidence—shows scale-dependent effects: it hurts performance at 1.7B but becomes roughly neutral at larger scales. Gradient-based methods like GRPO and DPO create competition between extraction and evidence generation, while RSFT sidesteps this through output filtering instead of gradient pressure. RSFT also shows much lower variance across training seeds compared to other approaches.

At 1.7B, my RSFT model achieves performance comparable to an 8B SFT baseline while using far fewer parameters, and the model rankings remain consistent across all tested scales.
```

{
  "command": "cd /home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_6/projects/faith-evidence-ie/docs/paper/sections && python3 -c \"\nimport re\nwith open('abstract.tex') as f:\n    text = f.read()\n# Remove \\begin{abstract} and \\end{abstract}\ntext = text.replace(r'\\begin{abstract}', '').replace(r'\\end{abstract}', '')\n# Replace --- with em-dash (one token)\ntext = text.replace('---', '—')\n# Replace ~ with space\ntext = text.replace('~', ' ')\n# Remove inline math but keep content as words where applicable\n# First handle simple math like \\$-\\$5.63pp -> count the number part as a word\ntext = re.sub(r'\\\\\\$', '$', text)  # literal \\$ -> $\n# Replace LaTeX commands that produce symbols\ntext = text.replace(r'\\times', '×')\ntext = text.replace(r'\\geq', '≥')\ntext = text.replace(r'\\rho', 'ρ')\ntext = text.replace(r'\\chi', 'χ')\n# Remove remaining LaTeX commands like \\textbf{...} -> keep content\ntext = re.sub(r'\\\\[a-zA-Z]+\\{([^}]*)\\}', r'\\1', text)\n# Remove remaining backslash commands\ntext = re.sub(r'\\\\[a-zA-Z]+', '', text)\n# Remove $ signs (math delimiters)\ntext = text.replace('$', '')\n# Remove braces\ntext = text.replace('{', '').replace('}', '')\n# Split and count non-empty tokens\nwords = [w for w in text.split() if w.strip()]\nprint(f'Word count: {len(words)}')\nprint()\nprint('All words:')\nfor i, w in enumerate(words):\n    print(f'{i+1}: {w}')\n\"\n",
  "description": "Accurate abstract word count with LaTeX rendering"
}

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01GPKorMjQ5Qz82knk8qWiJR`</summary>

````text
1	---
2	description: 学术论文图表设计与生成——四层指南（防丑原则 + 叙事原则 + Pattern 库 + 工具路由）。Paper Agent 规划 visualization_plan 时读，Worker 执行时也读。
3	name: paper-visualization
4	scope: global
5	tags:
6	- research
7	- visualization
8	- paper
9	- matplotlib
10	- paperbanana
11	version: 5
12	roles:
13	- agent
14	- paper_agent
15	---
16	
17	# Paper Visualization — 学术图表设计与生成
18	
19	> **核心理念**：好图不是"画得对"，是"回答一个问题"。每张图动笔前先问：**这张图要让读者看到什么？**
20	>
21	> **质量底线**：每张图必须达到 NeurIPS/ICML/EMNLP best paper 的视觉水准——高级感、专业感、精致感。绝不接受 matplotlib 默认样式的廉价感。
22	
23	本 skill 分五层：
24	
25	- **L0 学习同行** — 设计前看几张优秀同类型论文图，比读任何原则都有效
26	- **L1 防丑原则** — 底线，所有图必须遵守
27	- **L2 叙事原则** — 让图有说服力（区分"数据图"和"论证图"）
28	- **L3 Pattern 库** — 具体技法目录，按 `design_notes` 选用
29	- **L4 工具路由 + 模板** — paperbanana vs matplotlib，常用统计图模板
30	
31	---
32	
33	## L0 学习同行（设计前的功课）
34	
35	你的子领域里，**最被引用、最被记住的论文是怎么画图的？** 在动笔之前花点时间去看一眼——这个动作的回报远高于读完下面的 L1-L4。
36	
37	### 为什么这一步是重要的
38	
39	- 同领域读者已经被这些论文的视觉语言"训练"过——遵循惯例 = 降低读者认知负担
40	- 顶会论文经历过 reviewer 严苛审查，能登出来的图基本说明它的 narrative 设计是被接受的
41	- 抽象原则告诉你"用 fill_between 表示发散"，但好论文告诉你"在这种数据上，发散到底应该画多淡才合适"
42	- 一个领域往往有它特有的图表惯例（如校准研究的 reliability diagram、scaling 研究的 log-log curve、RL 的 learning curve with seed bands），不看就不会用
43	
44	### 什么时候值得投入
45	
46	判断权交给你。一些可参考的信号：
47	
48	- **值得多花时间**：会决定论文成败的图（hero figure、main result、新颖的实验类型）
49	- **不太值得**：常规 supplementary 图、loss curve 这类标准化已经很高的图
50	- **强烈建议看**：你打算画一种自己不熟悉的图，或者数据形态是这个领域少见的
51	
52	### 怎么看
53	
54	工具：`paper-image-extractor` skill 可以从 arXiv source 或 PDF 提取参考论文里的 figure。你已经有 `literature_map.yaml` 里的论文清单作为起点。
55	
56	看的时候问自己（不是机械检查清单，是真的去理解）：
57	
58	- 这张图回答了什么问题？读者一眼能 get 到吗？
59	- 它用了哪些叙事层（background band / 参考线 / 概念曲线 / fill 区域）？这些层在我的数据上有对应物吗？
60	- 它的颜色和 marker 怎么承载语义？我的图能不能借鉴这套映射？
61	- 它有什么是这篇论文特有的（与他们的具体数据 / claim 绑死）？这些不要照搬。
62	- 如果让我重画它的图，我会怎么改？为什么作者没这么做？
63	
64	### 怎么把学到的传下去
65	
66	把你看完后的判断**直接写进对应 fig 的 `design_notes`**，用人话不用模板。例子：
67	
68	```yaml
69	design_notes:
70	  - "学 GPTQ Fig 4：用 log-scale bit-width + dashed FP16 baseline + 不同 marker shape 分模型族"
71	  - "Pattern A（regime band）划分 in-domain / OOD 两段"
72	  - "AWQ Fig 2 那种 hatching 区分 outlier 不适用我们的数据，跳过"
73	```
74	
75	Worker 拿到这种 design_notes 就知道既要参考 GPTQ 的 layout 又要用 Pattern A，能直接落实成代码。
76	
77	### 边界
78	
79	- 不要复刻，要消化。直接抄一张图既没用（数据不一样）也有风险（与他们的 claim 强绑定）
80	- 不要看太多。3 张就够，多了 Paper Agent 会陷入"该听谁的"
81	- 看不到原图（提取失败 / arXiv 无 source）就跳过，不要在这里卡住流程
82	
83	---
84	
85	## L1 防丑原则（底线，不可违反）
86	
87	来自 Tufte 和 Luís Cruz 16 条公认规范。Agent 默认遵守，无需逐条思考。
88	
89	1. **Self-explanatory** — 不看正文就能看懂：title/caption + 所有轴有 label + 单位
90	2. **去 chartjunk** — 不要 3D、不要背景框、不要无意义网格、不要 dual y-axis、不要 pie/donut chart
91	3. **直接标注 > Legend** — 能直接标在曲线/柱旁就不用 legend；legend 必要时不遮数据
92	4. **颜色/形状/纹理只用于 data variation** — 不用颜色装饰，不用相同颜色表示不同数据
93	5. **Meaningful baseline** — bar chart 必须从 0 起；scatter 可以非 0
94	6. **文字水平** — label 不要旋转/垂直；类别名太长 → 用 horizontal bar chart
95	7. **Sort for comparison** — 柱状图按数值排序，便于对比（除非有自然顺序如时间）
96	8. **字号一致** — 用 mm 计算 figsize（`ONE_MM = 1/25.4`），不在 LaTeX 里 `\includegraphics[scale=...]` resize
97	9. **色盲友好** — 默认 Okabe-Ito；红/绿区分时变 saturation；考虑 grayscale 打印
98	10. **Bold 只用于强调** — 不要全图加粗；highlight 留给 takeaway
99	11. **Line plot 只用于有序数据** — 类别变量用 bar
100	12. **单 x 单 y 轴** — 必须双轴时改成 multi-panel
101	13. **300 DPI、PDF 矢量、savefig.bbox=tight、去掉 top/right spines**
102	
103	---
104	
105	## L2 叙事原则（让图变成论证）
106	
107	L1 让图"不丑"，L2 让图"有说服力"。这一层是 Agent 自主画图时最容易缺失的。
108	
109	### 原则 1：每张图回答一个明确问题
110	
111	动笔前用一句话写下："This figure shows that ___."
112	
113	如果写不出来，就不要画。如果写出来的 takeaway 不是论文的 claim，就要重新设计。
114	
115	**反例**：`"Accuracy across methods"` — 这只是个标题，不是问题
116	**正例**：`"FP16 baseline 在小模型上比量化版高 5pp，但 70B 模型上差距消失"` — 这是一张图要说服读者相信的事
117	
118	`visualization_plan.{fig_id}.narrative_intent` 字段写这一句。
119	
120	### 原则 2：颜色绑定语义而非数据顺序
121	
122	错的做法：`colors = ['blue', 'orange', 'green', 'red']` 按数据顺序分配。
123	对的做法：`BN → 蓝色（始终）`、`non-BN → 橙色（始终）`、`collapse/danger → vermillion (#D55E00)`、`safe/baseline → blue (#0072B2)`。
124	
125	整篇论文 6 张图，BN 永远蓝、collapse 永远红——零认知负担，读者一眼建立映射。
126	
127	### 原则 3：信息层级用 visual variable 表达
128	
129	数据的"质量"和"分类"应该可见，不应该藏在 caption 里：
130	
131	| 信息 | Visual 表达 |
132	|------|------------|
133	| n < 50 低置信度点 | hollow marker + 标注 `n=` |
134	| Out-of-sample / pending 数据 | hollow + 不同 marker shape（如 diamond）+ "OOS" label |
135	| Collapse / 异常类别 | hatching（斜线填充）|
136	| 主结果 vs 次要结果 | 主用饱和色，次要用 muted 色或 alpha |
137	
138	### 原则 4：加叙事层，不只画数据
139	
140	数据是图的基础，叙事层是让读者"读懂"的关键。常见叙事层：
141	
142	- **Background bands** — `axhspan/axvspan` 标定性区域（"collapse zone"、"safe zone"、"Regime A/B/C"）
143	- **Fill_between** — 显示两条曲线发散区域（alpha 极低如 0.06，不抢眼但有面积）
144	- **Reference lines** — 锚定真实值（如 dashed line 标"actual W3A16 accuracy"）
145	- **Equivalence band** — `axvspan` gradient fade 标 +/-1pp 等价区间
146	- **Concept curve** — 完全合成的曲线（如 cosine trajectory）建立理论直觉
147	
148	这些都不来自数据，但帮读者"看到"数据想说什么。
149	
150	### 原则 5：Subtitle / panel label 写 insight 不写描述
151	
152	错：`"Layer 6 comparison across three models"`
153	对：`"Same relative position, opposite function"`
154	
155	错：`"Reliability diagram"`
156	对：`"Quantization shifts confidence but not accuracy"`
157	
158	副标题是免费的 takeaway，不要浪费。
159	
160	### 原则 6：Multi-panel 每个面板独立回答一个 sub-question
161	
162	不要做"主图 + 装饰小图"。每个 panel 必须有独立的信息价值。
163	
164	如果一个 panel 想不出独立的问题，要么删掉，要么合并到主 panel。
165	
166	布局上：非对称比例（如 1.4:1）让视觉重心对齐叙事重心。
167	
168	### 原则 7：所有图至少 3 轮迭代，Hero figure 至少 5 轮
169	
170	第一版画出来基本不能直接用。计划好预算：先确定数据和布局，再调样式标注，最后 polish。不要指望一次到位。
171	
172	### Hero Figure 定义
173	
174	Hero Figure = Figure 1 + 主结果图（main result figure）。这两类图对论文第一印象影响最大，必须投入更多迭代。
175	
176	---
177	
178	## L3 Pattern 库（按 `design_notes` 选用）
179	
180	按"图要回答的问题"选 pattern。每个 pattern 给出**何时用**、**怎么用**（具体代码或 matplotlib API）。
181	
182	### Pattern A：定性区域 / regime bands
183	
184	**何时用**：x 或 y 轴有自然分段（regime、phase、severity tier）。
185	
186	```python
187	# horizontal bands
188	for lo, hi, color, label in [(0, 1, '#EBF5FB', 'Regime A'), (1, 5, '#EAFAF1', 'Regime B')]:
189	    ax.axhspan(lo, hi, alpha=0.25, color=color, zorder=0)
190	    ax.text(x_label_pos, (lo+hi)/2, label, va='center', fontsize=9, color='gray')
191	
192	# 单一 collapse zone
193	ax.axhspan(-5, 5, color='#FDEDEC', alpha=0.5, zorder=0)
194	ax.text(0.02, 2.5, 'collapse zone', fontsize=8, color='#ccaaaa', fontstyle='italic')
195	```
196	
197	### Pattern B：发散面积 fill_between
198	
199	**何时用**：两组数据从某点开始发散，想让"发散程度"可见。
200	
201	```python
202	# alpha 0.06 极淡，不抢眼但有面积
203	ax.fill_between(x_range, curve_high, curve_low, alpha=0.06, color='#555555', zorder=1)
204	```
205	
206	### Pattern C：参考线锚定真实值
207	
208	**何时用**：合成 / 缩放后的 x 轴需要锚定到真实物理量。
209	
210	```python
211	ax.axhline(y=actual_value, color=color, linestyle=':', linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.3)
212	t = ax.text(x_pos, actual_value+1, 'actual W3A16', fontsize=7, color=color, alpha=0.5)
213	t.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])  # 防遮挡
214	```
215	
216	### Pattern D：信息层级 marker（hollow vs solid）
217	
218	**何时用**：同一图中有不同置信度 / OOS / pending 数据。
219	
220	```python
221	# 实心 = n >= 50, 空心 = n < 50
222	if n < 50:
223	    ax.plot(x, y, marker='o', markerfacecolor='white', markeredgecolor=color,
224	            markeredgewidth=1.2, markersize=6)
225	    ax.annotate(f'n={n}', (x, y), xytext=(0, 7), textcoords='offset points',
226	                fontsize=5, color=color, fontstyle='italic')
227	```
228	
229	### Pattern E：Wilson CI / bootstrap 不确定性
230	
231	**何时用**：accuracy / proportion 数据需要置信区间。
232	
233	```python
234	def wilson_ci(p, n, z=1.96):
235	    denom = 1 + z**2 / n
236	    center = (p + z**2 / (2*n)) / denom
237	    half = z * np.sqrt((p*(1-p) + z**2/(4*n)) / n) / denom
238	    return max(0, center - half), min(1, center + half)
239	```
240	
241	### Pattern F：Per-label offset dict（解决标注重叠）
242	
243	**何时用**：scatter 上多个点需要标注且自动布局重叠。
244	
245	```python
246	label_offsets = {
247	    'Qwen3-8B':  (6, -12),   # 向下避让
248	    'Qwen3-4B':  (-55, 4),   # 向左避让
249	    'Llama-8B':  (6, 6),
250	}
251	for name, (x, y) in data.items():
252	    ox, oy = label_offsets.get(name, (6, 6))
253	    ax.annotate(name, (x, y), xytext=(ox, oy), textcoords='offset points', fontsize=7)
254	```
255	
256	### Pattern G：Hatching 加分类维度
257	
258	**何时用**：已用颜色表示一个维度，再加一个二元维度（如 collapse vs not）。
259	
260	```python
261	for is_collapse, bar in zip(collapse_flags, bars):
262	    if is_collapse:
263	        bar.set_hatch('///')
264	        bar.set_alpha(0.7)
265	```
266	
267	### Pattern H：Multi-panel 非对称布局
268	
269	**何时用**：主图 + 辅助 panel；或两个等重要 panel 但视觉重心需要倾斜。
270	
271	```python
272	fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(6.5, 3.2),
273	                          gridspec_kw={'width_ratios': [1.4, 1]})
274	# 主面板 1.4x 宽，副面板 1x；视觉重心在左
275	```
276	
277	### Pattern I：Broken axis（异常值或对数 0）
278	
279	**何时用**：数据范围跨度大，或对数轴需要表示 0。
280	
281	```python
282	d = 0.012
283	kwargs = dict(transform=ax.transAxes, color='black', clip_on=False, linewidth=0.6)
284	ax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (-d, +d), **kwargs)  # 右下角斜线
285	ax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (1-d, 1+d), **kwargs)  # 右上角斜线
286	```
287	
288	### Pattern J：Path-effect 文字描边
289	
290	**何时用**：标注必须放在数据点 / colored region 上方。
291	
292	```python
293	import matplotlib.patheffects as pe
294	t = ax.text(x, y, 'label', fontsize=8, color='red')
295	t.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])
296	```
297	
298	### Pattern K：Concept curve（建立直觉）
299	
300	**何时用**：论文核心 claim 是定性现象，需要先让读者理解理论预期。
301	
302	```python
303	# 完全合成的 cosine trajectory，不是实验数据
304	x = np.linspace(0, 1, 100)
305	trajectory = baseline * (1 - np.cos(np.pi * x)) / 2
306	ax.plot(x, trajectory, linewidth=2, solid_capstyle='round')
307	ax.annotate('gap eliminated', (1.0, final_y), color='#388E3C',
308	            fontstyle='italic', fontsize=9)
309	```
310	
311	### Pattern L：Forest plot（meta-analysis 风格）
312	
313	**何时用**：多个子条件的 effect size + CI 对比，加 inline 统计数字。
314	
315	3-column GridSpec：(1) 标签 (2) CI 区间图 (3) 统计数字（mean, CI, p-value）
316	
317	每行：dot + horizontal line(CI) + 数字 inline。等价带用半透明 axvspan。
318	
319	### 概念图 L1 防丑原则（7 条）
320	
321	概念图（架构图、流程图、framework 示意）的视觉底线，与统计图 L1 互补。
322	
323	1. **Flow direction 一致** — 全图统一左→右或上→下，不混合方向
324	2. **Box 规格统一** — 同层级 box 尺寸、圆角、边框粗细一致
325	3. **箭头样式统一** — 不混用多种箭头类型（除非有语义区分）
326	4. **层级清晰** — 不同抽象层用缩进/嵌套/背景色区分
327	5. **密度控制** — 单张图 ≤15 个 box，超过则拆分或分组
328	6. **Label 完整** — 每个 box 和箭头都有文字说明
329	7. **无装饰性元素** — 不加纯装饰图标、渐变、3D 效果
330	
331	### 概念图 L2 叙事原则（6 条）
332	
333	让概念图不只是"画了个框图"，而是"引导读者理解系统"。
334	
335	1. **视觉锚点** — 核心贡献用饱和色/大字/加粗边框突出，其余 muted
336	2. **阅读路径** — 沿箭头方向自然读完全图，不需要跳跃
337	3. **对比布局** — 左右并列展示 before/after 或 baseline/ours
338	4. **分组嵌套** — 用浅色背景框将相关组件分组（如 encoder/decoder）
339	5. **箭头语义** — 实线=数据流，虚线=控制流/可选，粗线=关键路径
340	6. **信息层级** — 主路径用饱和色+大字，辅助信息用灰色+小字
341	
342	### 概念图 L3 Pattern 库
343	
344	| Pattern | 名称 | 适用场景 |
345	|---------|------|---------|
346	| P-A | 线性流程 | 数据处理 pipeline、推理流程 |
347	| P-B | 层级嵌套 | 模型架构（encoder/decoder/attention） |
348	| P-C | 对比并列 | baseline vs ours、before/after |
349	| P-D | 分支汇聚 | 多输入融合、ensemble |
350	| P-E | 循环 | 训练循环、迭代优化 |
351	| P-F | 诊断链 | 消融实验逻辑、排错流程 |
352	| P-G | 三面板综合 | 概念+数据+结论（常用于 Figure 1） |
353	
354	### 概念图 Checklist（7 项）
355	
356	- [ ] Flow direction 全图统一（左→右或上→下）
357	- [ ] Box 规格一致（同层级尺寸、圆角、边框相同）
358	- [ ] 视觉层级清晰（核心突出，辅助 muted）
359	- [ ] 核心贡献在视觉上最突出
360	- [ ] 箭头语义一致（实线/虚线/粗细有意义）
361	- [ ] 留白充足（box 之间不拥挤）
362	- [ ] 跨图风格一致（配色、字号、线宽与论文其他图统一）
363	
364	---
365	
366	## L4 工具路由 + 模板
367	
368	### 路由规则
369	
370	| 场景 | 工具 | 原因 |
371	|------|------|------|
372	| 概念图（架构图、流程图、framework 示意） | **PaperBanana（Gemini）** | matplotlib 手写 boxes/arrows 极痛苦 |
373	| 统计图（scatter、bar、line、heatmap、forest） | **matplotlib** | 确定性、可控、有模板 |
374	| 离线 / 无 GOOGLE_API_KEY | **matplotlib** | 不依赖外部 API |
375	
376	项目级偏好：`config/projects/{name}.yaml` 的 `visualization.preferred_tool` 可覆盖。
377	
378	### Matplotlib 通用样式（rcParams 标准）
379	
380	```python
381	import matplotlib
382	matplotlib.use('Agg')
383	import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
384	import matplotlib.patheffects as pe
385	import numpy as np
386	
387	plt.rcParams.update({
388	    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',
389	    'font.size': 11, 'axes.titlesize': 13, 'axes.labelsize': 12,
390	    'xtick.labelsize': 10, 'ytick.labelsize': 10, 'legend.fontsize': 10,
391	    'figure.dpi': 300, 'savefig.dpi': 300,
392	    'savefig.bbox': 'tight', 'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,
393	    'axes.spines.top': False, 'axes.spines.right': False,
394	    'pdf.fonttype': 42, 'ps.fonttype': 42,  # TrueType 嵌入
395	    'lines.linewidth': 1.8,
396	})
397	
398	# 色盲友好语义色板（Okabe-Ito）
399	SEMANTIC_COLORS = {
400	    'baseline':   '#0072B2',  # blue - safe / mild
401	    'treatment':  '#E69F00',  # orange - intervention
402	    'collapse':   '#D55E00',  # vermillion - danger / catastrophic
403	    'safe':       '#009E73',  # green - good outcome
404	    'neutral':    '#999999',  # gray - reference / control
405	}
406	```
407	
408	### 常用模板（仅作起点，需按 narrative_intent 改造）
409	
410	详细执行模板见 `skills/global/draw-paper-figure.skill.md`：
411	- Reliability Diagram（校准图）
412	- ECE Heatmap（方法 × 数据集 ΔECE）
413	- T* Scatter Plot（任务 Regime 可视化）
414	
415	**重要**：这些模板是起点不是终点。每张图根据 `narrative_intent` 必须加 L3 中的相关 pattern，不能直接套用。
416	
417	### 主图（Figure 1）特别提示
418	
419	大多数 ML 论文的 Figure 1 是概念图/架构图/方法概览——正是 PaperBanana 的强项。除非 Figure 1 是纯数值结果对比（如 Pareto frontier），否则优先用 PaperBanana。Figure 1 的 `narrative_intent` 必须与 `writing_plan.yaml` 的 `meta.core_claim` 直接关联。
420	
421	### PaperBanana 用法
422	
423	见 `skills/global/paper-banana.skill.md`。意图描述时直接引用 `narrative_intent`，并把 L3 中的 pattern 名写进 intent（如 "with collapse zone background band, hollow markers for n<50"）。
424	
425	---
426	
427	## 给 Paper Agent 的填写规则
428	
429	规划 `visualization_plan` 时，对每张图填：
430	
431	```yaml
432	- fig_id: fig2_main_result
433	  type: statistical
434	  tool: matplotlib
435	  data_source: "registry: exp_001, exp_002"
436	  placement: experiments
437	
438	  # 新字段（必填）
439	  narrative_intent: "Quantized models match FP16 on accuracy but degrade calibration; ECE gap widens with bits→2."
440	  design_notes:
441	    - "Pattern A: regime bands for bits=8/4/3"
442	    - "Pattern C: dashed line for FP16 ECE baseline"
443	    - "Pattern D: hollow markers for n<50 data points"
444	
445	  aspect_ratio: "4:3"
446	```
447	
448	`narrative_intent`：一句话，主动语态，描述读者应该相信的事。
449	
450	`design_notes`：从 L3 选 0-4 个 pattern，给出选用理由。可以为空（说明默认模板足够）。
451	
452	---
453	
454	## 提交前 Checklist（以下为统计图专用）
455	
456	L1 部分（防丑底线）：
457	- [ ] 所有轴有 label + 单位
458	- [ ] 无 dual y-axis、无 3D、无 pie
459	- [ ] 颜色绑定语义、色盲友好
460	- [ ] Bar chart 从 0 起（如有）
461	- [ ] 字号 ≥ 10pt（缩到 column width 后仍可读）
462	- [ ] PDF 矢量、300 DPI
463	
464	L2 部分（叙事强度）：
465	- [ ] 能用一句话写出 figure takeaway（应等于 `narrative_intent`）
466	- [ ] Subtitle / panel label 是 insight，不是描述
467	- [ ] 数据质量差异在视觉上可见（n、OOS、pending 等）
468	- [ ] 至少考虑过是否需要 background band / fill_between / reference line（不一定要加）
469	
470	L3 部分（高级感——不可跳过）：
471	- [ ] 不像 matplotlib gallery 示例（无默认灰背景、无粗网格、无 tab10 色板）
472	- [ ] 配色 muted 和谐，不刺眼，不过度饱和
473	- [ ] 线宽、字号、间距全图统一
474	- [ ] 元素对齐严格（bars 底部对齐、labels 水平对齐）
475	- [ ] 留白充足（元素之间不拥挤）
476	- [ ] 与论文全文其他图的风格一致
477	- [ ] 终极判断：这张图放在 NeurIPS best paper 里违和吗？
478	
````

visualization\n- paper\n- matplotlib\n- paperbanana\nversion: 5\nroles:\n- agent\n- paper_agent\n---\n\n# Paper Visualization — 学术图表设计与生成\n\n> **核心理念**：好图不是\"画得对\"，是\"回答一个问题\"。每张图动笔前先问：**这张图要让读者看到什么？**\n>\n> **质量底线**：每张图必须达到 NeurIPS/ICML/EMNLP best paper 的视觉水准——高级感、专业感、精致感。绝不接受 matplotlib 默认样式的廉价感。\n\n本 skill 分五层：\n\n- **L0 学习同行** — 设计前看几张优秀同类型论文图，比读任何原则都有效\n- **L1 防丑原则** — 底线，所有图必须遵守\n- **L2 叙事原则** — 让图有说服力（区分\"数据图\"和\"论证图\"）\n- **L3 Pattern 库** — 具体技法目录，按 `design_notes` 选用\n- **L4 工具路由 + 模板** — paperbanana vs matplotlib，常用统计图模板\n\n---\n\n## L0 学习同行（设计前的功课）\n\n你的子领域里，**最被引用、最被记住的论文是怎么画图的？** 在动笔之前花点时间去看一眼——这个动作的回报远高于读完下面的 L1-L4。\n\n### 为什么这一步是重要的\n\n- 同领域读者已经被这些论文的视觉语言\"训练\"过——遵循惯例 = 降低读者认知负担\n- 顶会论文经历过 reviewer 严苛审查，能登出来的图基本说明它的 narrative 设计是被接受的\n- 抽象原则告诉你\"用 fill_between 表示发散\"，但好论文告诉你\"在这种数据上，发散到底应该画多淡才合适\"\n- 一个领域往往有它特有的图表惯例（如校准研究的 reliability diagram、scaling 研究的 log-log curve、RL 的 learning curve with seed bands），不看就不会用\n\n### 什么时候值得投入\n\n判断权交给你。一些可参考的信号：\n\n- **值得多花时间**：会决定论文成败的图（hero figure、main result、新颖的实验类型）\n- **不太值得**：常规 supplementary 图、loss curve 这类标准化已经很高的图\n- **强烈建议看**：你打算画一种自己不熟悉的图，或者数据形态是这个领域少见的\n\n### 怎么看\n\n工具：`paper-image-extractor` skill 可以从 arXiv source 或 PDF 提取参考论文里的 figure。你已经有 `literature_map.yaml` 里的论文清单作为起点。\n\n看的时候问自己（不是机械检查清单，是真的去理解）：\n\n- 这张图回答了什么问题？读者一眼能 get 到吗？\n- 它用了哪些叙事层（background band / 参考线 / 概念曲线 / fill 区域）？这些层在我的数据上有对应物吗？\n- 它的颜色和 marker 怎么承载语义？我的图能不能借鉴这套映射？\n- 它有什么是这篇论文特有的（与他们的具体数据 / claim 绑死）？这些不要照搬。\n- 如果让我重画它的图，我会怎么改？为什么作者没这么做？\n\n### 怎么把学到的传下去\n\n把你看完后的判断**直接写进对应 fig 的 `design_notes`**，用人话不用模板。例子：\n\n```yaml\ndesign_notes:\n  - \"学 GPTQ Fig 4：用 log-scale bit-width + dashed FP16 baseline + 不同 marker shape 分模型族\"\n  - \"Pattern A（regime band）划分 in-domain / OOD 两段\"\n  - \"AWQ Fig 2 那种 hatching 区分 outlier 不适用我们的数据，跳过\"\n```\n\nWorker 拿到这种 design_notes 就知道既要参考 GPTQ 的 layout 又要用 Pattern A，能直接落实成代码。\n\n### 边界\n\n- 不要复刻，要消化。直接抄一张图既没用（数据不一样）也有风险（与他们的 claim 强绑定）\n- 不要看太多。3 张就够，多了 Paper Agent 会陷入\"该听谁的\"\n- 看不到原图（提取失败 / arXiv 无 source）就跳过，不要在这里卡住流程\n\n---\n\n## L1 防丑原则（底线，不可违反）\n\n来自 Tufte 和 Luís Cruz 16 条公认规范。Agent 默认遵守，无需逐条思考。\n\n1. **Self-explanatory** — 不看正文就能看懂：title/caption + 所有轴有 label + 单位\n2. **去 chartjunk** — 不要 3D、不要背景框、不要无意义网格、不要 dual y-axis、不要 pie/donut chart\n3. **直接标注 > Legend** — 能直接标在曲线/柱旁就不用 legend；legend 必要时不遮数据\n4. **颜色/形状/纹理只用于 data variation** — 不用颜色装饰，不用相同颜色表示不同数据\n5. **Meaningful baseline** — bar chart 必须从 0 起；scatter 可以非 0\n6. **文字水平** — label 不要旋转/垂直；类别名太长 → 用 horizontal bar chart\n7. **Sort for comparison** — 柱状图按数值排序，便于对比（除非有自然顺序如时间）\n8. **字号一致** — 用 mm 计算 figsize（`ONE_MM = 1/25.4`），不在 LaTeX 里 `\\includegraphics[scale=...]` resize\n9. **色盲友好** — 默认 Okabe-Ito；红/绿区分时变 saturation；考虑 grayscale 打印\n10. **Bold 只用于强调** — 不要全图加粗；highlight 留给 takeaway\n11. **Line plot 只用于有序数据** — 类别变量用 bar\n12. **单 x 单 y 轴** — 必须双轴时改成 multi-panel\n13. **300 DPI、PDF 矢量、savefig.bbox=tight、去掉 top/right spines**\n\n---\n\n## L2 叙事原则（让图变成论证）\n\nL1 让图\"不丑\"，L2 让图\"有说服力\"。这一层是 Agent 自主画图时最容易缺失的。\n\n### 原则 1：每张图回答一个明确问题\n\n动笔前用一句话写下：\"This figure shows that ___.\"\n\n如果写不出来，就不要画。如果写出来的 takeaway 不是论文的 claim，就要重新设计。\n\n**反例**：`\"Accuracy across methods\"` — 这只是个标题，不是问题\n**正例**：`\"FP16 baseline 在小模型上比量化版高 5pp，但 70B 模型上差距消失\"` — 这是一张图要说服读者相信的事\n\n`visualization_plan.{fig_id}.narrative_intent` 字段写这一句。\n\n### 原则 2：颜色绑定语义而非数据顺序\n\n错的做法：`colors = ['blue', 'orange', 'green', 'red']` 按数据顺序分配。\n对的做法：`BN → 蓝色（始终）`、`non-BN → 橙色（始终）`、`collapse/danger → vermillion (#D55E00)`、`safe/baseline → blue (#0072B2)`。\n\n整篇论文 6 张图，BN 永远蓝、collapse 永远红——零认知负担，读者一眼建立映射。\n\n### 原则 3：信息层级用 visual variable 表达\n\n数据的\"质量\"和\"分类\"应该可见，不应该藏在 caption 里：\n\n| 信息 | Visual 表达 |\n|------|------------|\n| n < 50 低置信度点 | hollow marker + 标注 `n=` |\n| Out-of-sample / pending 数据 | hollow + 不同 marker shape（如 diamond）+ \"OOS\" label |\n| Collapse / 异常类别 | hatching（斜线填充）|\n| 主结果 vs 次要结果 | 主用饱和色，次要用 muted 色或 alpha |\n\n### 原则 4：加叙事层，不只画数据\n\n数据是图的基础，叙事层是让读者\"读懂\"的关键。常见叙事层：\n\n- **Background bands** — `axhspan/axvspan` 标定性区域（\"collapse zone\"、\"safe zone\"、\"Regime A/B/C\"）\n- **Fill_between** — 显示两条曲线发散区域（alpha 极低如 0.06，不抢眼但有面积）\n- **Reference lines** — 锚定真实值（如 dashed line 标\"actual W3A16 accuracy\"）\n- **Equivalence band** — `axvspan` gradient fade 标 +/-1pp 等价区间\n- **Concept curve** — 完全合成的曲线（如 cosine trajectory）建立理论直觉\n\n这些都不来自数据，但帮读者\"看到\"数据想说什么。\n\n### 原则 5：Subtitle / panel label 写 insight 不写描述\n\n错：`\"Layer 6 comparison across three models\"`\n对：`\"Same relative position, opposite function\"`\n\n错：`\"Reliability diagram\"`\n对：`\"Quantization shifts confidence but not accuracy\"`\n\n副标题是免费的 takeaway，不要浪费。\n\n### 原则 6：Multi-panel 每个面板独立回答一个 sub-question\n\n不要做\"主图 + 装饰小图\"。每个 panel 必须有独立的信息价值。\n\n如果一个 panel 想不出独立的问题，要么删掉，要么合并到主 panel。\n\n布局上：非对称比例（如 1.4:1）让视觉重心对齐叙事重心。\n\n### 原则 7：所有图至少 3 轮迭代，Hero figure 至少 5 轮\n\n第一版画出来基本不能直接用。计划好预算：先确定数据和布局，再调样式标注，最后 polish。不要指望一次到位。\n\n### Hero Figure 定义\n\nHero Figure = Figure 1 + 主结果图（main result figure）。这两类图对论文第一印象影响最大，必须投入更多迭代。\n\n---\n\n## L3 Pattern 库（按 `design_notes` 选用）\n\n按\"图要回答的问题\"选 pattern。每个 pattern 给出**何时用**、**怎么用**（具体代码或 matplotlib API）。\n\n### Pattern A：定性区域 / regime bands\n\n**何时用**：x 或 y 轴有自然分段（regime、phase、severity tier）。\n\n```python\n# horizontal bands\nfor lo, hi, color, label in [(0, 1, '#EBF5FB', 'Regime A'), (1, 5, '#EAFAF1', 'Regime B')]:\n    ax.axhspan(lo, hi, alpha=0.25, color=color, zorder=0)\n    ax.text(x_label_pos, (lo+hi)/2, label, va='center', fontsize=9, color='gray')\n\n# 单一 collapse zone\nax.axhspan(-5, 5, color='#FDEDEC', alpha=0.5, zorder=0)\nax.text(0.02, 2.5, 'collapse zone', fontsize=8, color='#ccaaaa', fontstyle='italic')\n```\n\n### Pattern B：发散面积 fill_between\n\n**何时用**：两组数据从某点开始发散，想让\"发散程度\"可见。\n\n```python\n# alpha 0.06 极淡，不抢眼但有面积\nax.fill_between(x_range, curve_high, curve_low, alpha=0.06, color='#555555', zorder=1)\n```\n\n### Pattern C：参考线锚定真实值\n\n**何时用**：合成 / 缩放后的 x 轴需要锚定到真实物理量。\n\n```python\nax.axhline(y=actual_value, color=color, linestyle=':', linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.3)\nt = ax.text(x_pos, actual_value+1, 'actual W3A16', fontsize=7, color=color, alpha=0.5)\nt.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])  # 防遮挡\n```\n\n### Pattern D：信息层级 marker（hollow vs solid）\n\n**何时用**：同一图中有不同置信度 / OOS / pending 数据。\n\n```python\n# 实心 = n >= 50, 空心 = n < 50\nif n < 50:\n    ax.plot(x, y, marker='o', markerfacecolor='white', markeredgecolor=color,\n            markeredgewidth=1.2, markersize=6)\n    ax.annotate(f'n={n}', (x, y), xytext=(0, 7), textcoords='offset points',\n                fontsize=5, color=color, fontstyle='italic')\n```\n\n### Pattern E：Wilson CI / bootstrap 不确定性\n\n**何时用**：accuracy / proportion 数据需要置信区间。\n\n```python\ndef wilson_ci(p, n, z=1.96):\n    denom = 1 + z**2 / n\n    center = (p + z**2 / (2*n)) / denom\n    half = z * np.sqrt((p*(1-p) + z**2/(4*n)) / n) / denom\n    return max(0, center - half), min(1, center + half)\n```\n\n### Pattern F：Per-label offset dict（解决标注重叠）\n\n**何时用**：scatter 上多个点需要标注且自动布局重叠。\n\n```python\nlabel_offsets = {\n    'Qwen3-8B':  (6, -12),   # 向下避让\n    'Qwen3-4B':  (-55, 4),   # 向左避让\n    'Llama-8B':  (6, 6),\n}\nfor name, (x, y) in data.items():\n    ox, oy = label_offsets.get(name, (6, 6))\n    ax.annotate(name, (x, y), xytext=(ox, oy), textcoords='offset points', fontsize=7)\n```\n\n### Pattern G：Hatching 加分类维度\n\n**何时用**：已用颜色表示一个维度，再加一个二元维度（如 collapse vs not）。\n\n```python\nfor is_collapse, bar in zip(collapse_flags, bars):\n    if is_collapse:\n        bar.set_hatch('///')\n        bar.set_alpha(0.7)\n```\n\n### Pattern H：Multi-panel 非对称布局\n\n**何时用**：主图 + 辅助 panel；或两个等重要 panel 但视觉重心需要倾斜。\n\n```python\nfig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(6.5, 3.2),\n                          gridspec_kw={'width_ratios': [1.4, 1]})\n# 主面板 1.4x 宽，副面板 1x；视觉重心在左\n```\n\n### Pattern I：Broken axis（异常值或对数 0）\n\n**何时用**：数据范围跨度大，或对数轴需要表示 0。\n\n```python\nd = 0.012\nkwargs = dict(transform=ax.transAxes, color='black', clip_on=False, linewidth=0.6)\nax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (-d, +d), **kwargs)  # 右下角斜线\nax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (1-d, 1+d), **kwargs)  # 右上角斜线\n```\n\n### Pattern J：Path-effect 文字描边\n\n**何时用**：标注必须放在数据点 / colored region 上方。\n\n```python\nimport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\nt = ax.text(x, y, 'label', fontsize=8, color='red')\nt.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])\n```\n\n### Pattern K：Concept curve（建立直觉）\n\n**何时用**：论文核心 claim 是定性现象，需要先让读者理解理论预期。\n\n```python\n# 完全合成的 cosine trajectory，不是实验数据\nx = np.linspace(0, 1, 100)\ntrajectory = baseline * (1 - np.cos(np.pi * x)) / 2\nax.plot(x, trajectory, linewidth=2, solid_capstyle='round')\nax.annotate('gap eliminated', (1.0, final_y), color='#388E3C',\n            fontstyle='italic', fontsize=9)\n```\n\n### Pattern L：Forest plot（meta-analysis 风格）\n\n**何时用**：多个子条件的 effect size + CI 对比，加 inline 统计数字。\n\n3-column GridSpec：(1) 标签 (2) CI 区间图 (3) 统计数字（mean, CI, p-value）\n\n每行：dot + horizontal line(CI) + 数字 inline。等价带用半透明 axvspan。\n\n### 概念图 L1 防丑原则（7 条）\n\n概念图（架构图、流程图、framework 示意）的视觉底线，与统计图 L1 互补。\n\n1. **Flow direction 一致** — 全图统一左→右或上→下，不混合方向\n2. **Box 规格统一** — 同层级 box 尺寸、圆角、边框粗细一致\n3. **箭头样式统一** — 不混用多种箭头类型（除非有语义区分）\n4. **层级清晰** — 不同抽象层用缩进/嵌套/背景色区分\n5. **密度控制** — 单张图 ≤15 个 box，超过则拆分或分组\n6. **Label 完整** — 每个 box 和箭头都有文字说明\n7. **无装饰性元素** — 不加纯装饰图标、渐变、3D 效果\n\n### 概念图 L2 叙事原则（6 条）\n\n让概念图不只是\"画了个框图\"，而是\"引导读者理解系统\"。\n\n1. **视觉锚点** — 核心贡献用饱和色/大字/加粗边框突出，其余 muted\n2. **阅读路径** — 沿箭头方向自然读完全图，不需要跳跃\n3. **对比布局** — 左右并列展示 before/after 或 baseline/ours\n4. **分组嵌套** — 用浅色背景框将相关组件分组（如 encoder/decoder）\n5. **箭头语义** — 实线=数据流，虚线=控制流/可选，粗线=关键路径\n6. **信息层级** — 主路径用饱和色+大字，辅助信息用灰色+小字\n\n### 概念图 L3 Pattern 库\n\n| Pattern | 名称 | 适用场景 |\n|---------|------|---------|\n| P-A | 线性流程 | 数据处理 pipeline、推理流程 |\n| P-B | 层级嵌套 | 模型架构（encoder/decoder/attention） |\n| P-C | 对比并列 | baseline vs ours、before/after |\n| P-D | 分支汇聚 | 多输入融合、ensemble |\n| P-E | 循环 | 训练循环、迭代优化 |\n| P-F | 诊断链 | 消融实验逻辑、排错流程 |\n| P-G | 三面板综合 | 概念+数据+结论（常用于 Figure 1） |\n\n### 概念图 Checklist（7 项）\n\n- [ ] Flow direction 全图统一（左→右或上→下）\n- [ ] Box 规格一致（同层级尺寸、圆角、边框相同）\n- [ ] 视觉层级清晰（核心突出，辅助 muted）\n- [ ] 核心贡献在视觉上最突出\n- [ ] 箭头语义一致（实线/虚线/粗细有意义）\n- [ ] 留白充足（box 之间不拥挤）\n- [ ] 跨图风格一致（配色、字号、线宽与论文其他图统一）\n\n---\n\n## L4 工具路由 + 模板\n\n### 路由规则\n\n| 场景 | 工具 | 原因 |\n|------|------|------|\n| 概念图（架构图、流程图、framework 示意） | **PaperBanana（Gemini）** | matplotlib 手写 boxes/arrows 极痛苦 |\n| 统计图（scatter、bar、line、heatmap、forest） | **matplotlib** | 确定性、可控、有模板 |\n| 离线 / 无 GOOGLE_API_KEY | **matplotlib** | 不依赖外部 API |\n\n项目级偏好：`config/projects/{name}.yaml` 的 `visualization.preferred_tool` 可覆盖。\n\n### Matplotlib 通用样式（rcParams 标准）\n\n```python\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\nimport numpy as np\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',\n    'font.size': 11, 'axes.titlesize': 13, 'axes.labelsize': 12,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 10, 'ytick.labelsize': 10, 'legend.fontsize': 10,\n    'figure.dpi': 300, 'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight', 'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,\n    'axes.spines.top': False, 'axes.spines.right': False,\n    'pdf.fonttype': 42, 'ps.fonttype': 42,  # TrueType 嵌入\n    'lines.linewidth': 1.8,\n})\n\n# 色盲友好语义色板（Okabe-Ito）\nSEMANTIC_COLORS = {\n    'baseline':   '#0072B2',  # blue - safe / mild\n    'treatment':  '#E69F00',  # orange - intervention\n    'collapse':   '#D55E00',  # vermillion - danger / catastrophic\n    'safe':       '#009E73',  # green - good outcome\n    'neutral':    '#999999',  # gray - reference / control\n}\n```\n\n### 常用模板（仅作起点，需按 narrative_intent 改造）\n\n详细执行模板见 `skills/global/draw-paper-figure.skill.md`：\n- Reliability Diagram（校准图）\n- ECE Heatmap（方法 × 数据集 ΔECE）\n- T* Scatter Plot（任务 Regime 可视化）\n\n**重要**：这些模板是起点不是终点。每张图根据 `narrative_intent` 必须加 L3 中的相关 pattern，不能直接套用。\n\n### 主图（Figure 1）特别提示\n\n大多数 ML 论文的 Figure 1 是概念图/架构图/方法概览——正是 PaperBanana 的强项。除非 Figure 1 是纯数值结果对比（如 Pareto frontier），否则优先用 PaperBanana。Figure 1 的 `narrative_intent` 必须与 `writing_plan.yaml` 的 `meta.core_claim` 直接关联。\n\n### PaperBanana 用法\n\n见 `skills/global/paper-banana.skill.md`。意图描述时直接引用 `narrative_intent`，并把 L3 中的 pattern 名写进 intent（如 \"with collapse zone background band, hollow markers for n<50\"）。\n\n---\n\n## 给 Paper Agent 的填写规则\n\n规划 `visualization_plan` 时，对每张图填：\n\n```yaml\n- fig_id: fig2_main_result\n  type: statistical\n  tool: matplotlib\n  data_source: \"registry: exp_001, exp_002\"\n  placement: experiments\n\n  # 新字段（必填）\n  narrative_intent: \"Quantized models match FP16 on accuracy but degrade calibration; ECE gap widens with bits→2.\"\n  design_notes:\n    - \"Pattern A: regime bands for bits=8/4/3\"\n    - \"Pattern C: dashed line for FP16 ECE baseline\"\n    - \"Pattern D: hollow markers for n<50 data points\"\n\n  aspect_ratio: \"4:3\"\n```\n\n`narrative_intent`：一句话，主动语态，描述读者应该相信的事。\n\n`design_notes`：从 L3 选 0-4 个 pattern，给出选用理由。可以为空（说明默认模板足够）。\n\n---\n\n## 提交前 Checklist（以下为统计图专用）\n\nL1 部分（防丑底线）：\n- [ ] 所有轴有 label + 单位\n- [ ] 无 dual y-axis、无 3D、无 pie\n- [ ] 颜色绑定语义、色盲友好\n- [ ] Bar chart 从 0 起（如有）\n- [ ] 字号 ≥ 10pt（缩到 column width 后仍可读）\n- [ ] PDF 矢量、300 DPI\n\nL2 部分（叙事强度）：\n- [ ] 能用一句话写出 figure takeaway（应等于 `narrative_intent`）\n- [ ] Subtitle / panel label 是 insight，不是描述\n- [ ] 数据质量差异在视觉上可见（n、OOS、pending 等）\n- [ ] 至少考虑过是否需要 background band / fill_between / reference line（不一定要加）\n\nL3 部分（高级感——不可跳过）：\n- [ ] 不像 matplotlib gallery 示例（无默认灰背景、无粗网格、无 tab10 色板）\n- [ ] 配色 muted 和谐，不刺眼，不过度饱和\n- [ ] 线宽、字号、间距全图统一\n- [ ] 元素对齐严格（bars 底部对齐、labels 水平对齐）\n- [ ] 留白充足（元素之间不拥挤）\n- [ ] 与论文全文其他图的风格一致\n- [ ] 终极判断：这张图放在 The user wants a more polished/beautiful version. 

First I'll verify the Google API key is set up and check that PaperBanana is installed, then craft a detailed prompt describing the figure layout and styling I want.

CED scoring\\n\\n### 新 3 贡献\\n1. **Evidence-grounded DocRE + RSFT with NLI-based scoring** — 合并原 C1+C4。CED 不再作为独立贡献，NLI-based scoring（CED/flat-NLI 等效）是 RSFT 有效的关键组件\\n2. **Decoupling spectrum（观察性，非因果）** — 原 C3 降级为观察性描述。\\\"co-occurs with\\\" 替代 \\\"acts as amplifier\\\"。EDCR 作为分析工具嵌入此贡献（不独立列出）\\n3. **Evidence tax landscape + RSFT variance compression** — 原 C5 提升为正式贡献。Within-family threshold（1.7B 获益，4B+ 中性）+ cross-architecture variation（Qwen 8B ≈0 vs LLaMA 8B evidence helpful）+ RSFT 跨 scale 压缩 seed 方差。**Variance compression 是 C3 核心**（最稳固的 sub-finding，不受 M027 修正影响）。⚠️ **D155 W1 降级**: Abstract/Contributions 用 \\\"consistently reduces\\\" 定性表述 + sign test (4/4 scales 同方向, p=0.0625 n=4 结构性上限)，不再给具体倍数。分析正文保留 per-scale 数字 (1.7B 31×, 4B 7.5×, 8B 6.5×, LLaMA 4.6×) + χ² CI caveat\\n\\n### 关键 claim 调整\\n- 所有因果 claim → 观察性描述（GRPO \\\"acts as catastrophic amplifier\\\" → \\\"co-occurs with\\\"）\\n- EDCR ρ 从 -0.965 (n=15) 改为去重后数值（待 Agent 计算完成）\\n- \\\"+8pp\\\" → matched@2048 fair subset: 1.7B=-5.63pp, 4B=+0.73pp, 8B=+0.04pp（D144 corrected）。Abstract 加 \\\"on a controlled evaluation subset\\\"\\n- \\\"matches 8B SFT\\\" → \\\"approaches 8B SFT\\\"\\n- CED vs flat-NLI 不再区分，统称 \\\"NLI-based scoring\\\"\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n\\n- Primary: **EMNLP 2026 Main** (ARR July cycle, ~July 15-25 → commit EMNLP Aug/Sep)\\n- Fallback: AAAI 2027 (abstract July 25, paper Aug 1)\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\n**统一主线 (D131)**: Evidence generation in LLM-based DocRE is not a fixed cost — its role depends on **model capacity** (scale-dependent tax), **training paradigm** (decoupling spectrum), and **data quality** (NLI-based filtering). ```text
I have the paper framing context. Now I need to:
1. Get the GOOGLE_API_KEY
2. Write a detailed intent file for PaperBanana
3. Run PaperBanana to generate Figure 1

The current Figure 1 has two parts:
- Top: Document → LLM → Standard DocRE vs Evidence-Grounded DocRE comparison
- Bottom: RSFT pipeline

Let me write a much more polished intent description. The key narrative is: evidence-grounded DocRE as structured generation, with RSFT as the training method that avoids decoupling.


I need to retrieve the GOOGLE_API_KEY that was stored in a previous session when PaperBanana was used to redraw Figure 1. Since I'm working as Director, I'll need to access the stored secrets directly rather than using the Agent's request tools. I need to figure out how to access the GOOGLE_API_KEY since I can run PaperBanana directly on this EC2 instance. I have a `list_secrets` tool that only shows metadata, but the actual secret retrieval requires `request_secret` which I don't have access to as a Director. My best option is to dispatch this to the Main Agent who has the proper tools to retrieve the key and run PaperBanana.
```

Read 生成的 PNG 做视觉检查\n6. 如果满意，转 PDF：\n```python\nimport matplotlib; matplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt, matplotlib.image as mpimg\nimg = mpimg.imread('figures/paper/fig_overview.png')\nh, w = img.shape[:2]\nfig = plt.figure(figsize=(w/300, h/300), dpi=300)\nax = fig.add_axes([0,0,1,1]); ax.imshow(img); ax.axis('off')\nplt.savefig('figures/paper/fig_overview.pdf', bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0)\nplt.close()\n```\n7. 不满意则修改 `/tmp/fig1_intent.txt` 重新生成（至少 3 轮外部迭代）\n\n**关键要求**：\n- 不要加标题（NO TITLE）\n- Entity legend 用实体名（John/London/Oxford/England），不要用类型名（Person/Location）\n- \"Evidence-Grounded DocRE (Ours)\" 卡片必须是视觉最突出的元素\n- 完成后汇报结果并附截图描述",
  "project": "faith-evidence-ie"
}

Labels: John, London, Oxford, England. NOT \"Person\", \"Location\", etc.\n\nCENTER — LLM Box:\nRounded rectangle (corner radius 10px), solid fill #4A90D9 (medium blue, NOT too dark), subtle shadow (2px, 10% opacity).\n\"LLM\" centered, 13pt bold, white.\n\"(Qwen3 / LLaMA)\" below, 7pt, white, slightly transparent.\n\nArrow from Document to LLM: thin gray #999999 (1pt), small neat arrowhead.\n\nRIGHT — Two Output Cards stacked vertically with ~12px gap:\n\nTop card — Standard DocRE:\n\"Standard DocRE\" label above the card, 8pt, gray #888888.\nRounded rectangle, facecolor #F5F5F5, DASHED border #BBBBBB (1pt), corner radius 6px. This is the MOST PROMINENT visual element.\nInside, 8pt monospace, dark #333333:\n  (John, place_of_birth, London) | Evidence: [S1]\n  (John, educated_at, Oxford) | Evidence: [S2]\n  (London, country, England) | Evidence: [S1][S3]\n\"Evidence: [S1]\" etc. in bold blue #0072B2.\n\nArrow hierarchy (critical for visual storytelling):\n  LLM -> Standard DocRE: thin DASHED gray #CCCCCC (0.8pt), small arrowhead\n  LLM -> Evidence-Grounded (Ours): solid blue #0072B2 (1.5pt), slightly larger arrowhead\n\n=== BOTTOM STRIP: RSFT Pipeline ===\n\nFull-width rounded rectangle, facecolor #FFF8F0 (warm cream), border #D4A574 (1pt), corner radius 8px.\n\n\"Rejection Sampling Fine-Tuning (RSFT)\" top-left inside the strip, 9pt bold, #8B6914.\n\nFive pipeline boxes in a horizontal row, evenly spaced, same height (~35px):\n  1. \"SFT Model\" — facecolor #D0E8F2, border #0072B2 (1pt), rounded 6px\n  2. \"Sample N\" (line break) \"Responses\" — facecolor white, border #BBBBBB, rounded 6px\n  3. \"CED / NLI\" (line break) \"Score\" — facecolor white, border #BBBBBB, rounded 6px\n  4. \"Select\" (line break) \"Top-K\" — facecolor white, border #BBBBBB, rounded 6px\n  5. \"Fine-tune\" — facecolor #D0E8F2, border #0072B2 (1pt), rounded 6px, bold text\n\nArrows between boxes: thin gray #888888 (1pt), small arrowheads.\nCurved dashed return arrow below from Fine-tune back to SFT Model: blue #0072B2 (1pt), dashed.\n\"iterate\" label centered below the return arrow arc, 8pt italic, #0072B2.\n\n=== STYLE CONSTRAINTS ===\n- 300 DPI, pure white background\n- NO title, NO panel labels\n- Serif font (Times New Roman) for body text, monospace for triplets\n- Primary accent: #0072B2 (blue). ```text
1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related_work}
3	
4	Document-level relation extraction (DocRE) requires reasoning across sentence boundaries to identify relations between entity pairs.
5	DocRED \citep{yao2019docred} established the first large-scale benchmark for this task, catalyzing a line of encoder-based methods that treat DocRE as classification over entity pair representations.
6	ATLOP \citep{zhou2021atlop} introduced adaptive thresholding for multi-label prediction and localized context pooling for entity representations.
7	A parallel thread incorporates sentence-level evidence into these architectures.
8	SAIS \citep{xiao2022sais} introduced evidence supervision as an auxiliary training signal and showed that joint evidence prediction improves extraction through data augmentation and ensemble inference.
9	DREEAM \citep{ma2023dreeam} extended this approach with dynamic memory-based evidence retrieval that guides attention to supporting sentences.
10	EIDER \citep{xie2022eider} jointly trains an evidence extractor and fuses evidence representations into the relation classifier.
11	In all three systems, evidence identification is binary sentence classification over a fixed candidate set, a formulation that adds no output complexity.
12	Recent work has recast DocRE for large language models.
13	AutoRE \citep{autore2024} fine-tunes LLMs with QLoRA using a relation-head-facts decomposition; \citet{xu2024lmrc} frame extraction as multi-choice reading comprehension; RelPrior \citep{wang2025relprior} uses relation priors to guide LLM-based extraction; GLiDRE \citep{zhang2025glidre} presents a generalist lightweight approach; Ru-LLM \citep{wang2025rullm} incorporates logical rule constraints; and \citet{chen2025docksrag} combine retrieval-augmented generation with structured knowledge.
14	These methods match or surpass encoder baselines but produce bare triplets without evidence. Among LLM-based generative methods at the 8B scale, our RSFT model (54.33 Ign-F1 on DocRED dev) outperforms AutoRE (52.14\footnote{AutoRE Ign-F1 is from the LLaMA3-8B reproduction by \citet{wang2025relprior}; the original paper reports only F1.}) and is slightly below RelPrior ($-$1.17pp), while being the only method that additionally provides sentence-level evidence grounding.
15	The critical distinction lies in computational nature: in SAIS and DREEAM, evidence retrieval is classification that regularizes the encoder without increasing output length; in LLM-based DocRE, evidence requires additional output tokens per triple, imposing costs that depend on model capacity and optimization strategy.
16	Whether this cost is inevitable or can be mitigated is the central question of our work.
17	
18	Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO; \citealt{shao2024deepseekmath}) eliminates the separate value network by computing advantages within groups.
19	Subsequent work addresses failure modes: DAPO \citep{yu2025dapo} prevents entropy collapse, Dr.\ GRPO \citep{liu2025drgrpo} corrects length bias, and multi-objective variants \citep{liu2026gdpo,ito2025mogrpo} handle competing reward signals.
20	Applications to structured IE include R1-RE \citep{dai2025r1re} and REAR \citep{wang2025rear}.
21	Direct Preference Optimization \citep{rafailov2023direct} reformulates RLHF as implicit reward maximization.
22	Our decoupling spectrum is distinct from catastrophic forgetting in RL alignment \citep{jin2025rl_panacea}, where models lose pre-trained capabilities across tasks; in our setting, extraction and evidence compete \emph{within} a single task, resembling negative transfer in multi-task learning \citep{ruder2017overview,standley2020tasks,yu2020gradient}, amplified by QLoRA's constrained parameter space \citep{gao2023scaling,moskovitz2024constrained}.
23	RSFT avoids this intra-task competition by operating on discrete outputs rather than gradients, extending the sample-and-select principle \citep{wang2023selfconsistency} to structured IE with joint quality scoring.
24	
25	Evidence faithfulness evaluation has received increasing attention: \citet{laban2022summac} re-visit NLI-based models for summarization inconsistency detection, \citet{li2025drift} propose dual-reward inference for faithful rationale generation, \citet{li2025srvf} use supervised rationale verification with contrastive feedback for relation extraction, and \citet{zhang2024infuse} introduce fine-grained NLI-based faithfulness metrics for summarization.
26	Our CED scoring builds on similar NLI-based principles but targets evidence-relation entailment rather than free-form rationales.
27	
28	\citet{zelikman2022star} introduced STaR for iterative self-improvement; \citet{dong2023raft} generalize this with reward-model-based sample filtering; recent analysis shows rejection sampling implicitly performs PPO-like optimization \citep{tang2025rsftppo}.
29	Our RSFT is a single-round variant applied to evidence-grounded DocRE with CED/NLI scoring as a domain-specific quality filter.
30	Because RSFT selects complete outputs rather than differentiating through a reward signal, it cannot exploit the extraction-evidence trade-off that gradient methods discover, inherently preserving joint quality.
31	
```

```text
1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	\subsection{Experimental Setup}
5	\label{sec:setup}
6	
7	We evaluate on DocRED~\citep{yao2019docred} (3,053 training / 998 dev documents, 96 relation types) with Re-DocRED~\citep{tan2022redocred} for cross-dataset validation. Our primary model is Qwen3-4B; we also train Qwen3-1.7B, 8B, and 14B to study scale effects, with LLaMA-3.1-8B for cross-family validation. All models use LoRA~\citep{hu2022lora} ($r{=}64$, $\alpha{=}128$) with structured output jointly producing triples and evidence. RSFT selects the top-$k{=}1$ of $N{=}8$ samples by CED score (\S\ref{sec:ced}); GRPO uses group sizes $G{=}4$ (QLoRA) and $G{=}8$ (bf16); DPO constructs preference pairs from CED-ranked outputs. We report Ign-F1 (primary; ignores training-set triples), Rel-F1, Evi-F1 (TP-only and joint per DREEAM protocol~\citep{ma2023dreeam}), and EDCR (\S\ref{sec:edcr}). Detailed hyperparameters appear in the Appendix.
8	
9	
10	\subsection{Main Results}
11	\label{sec:main_results}
12	
13	Table~\ref{tab:main} presents the primary comparison on Qwen3-4B. The SFT baseline exhibits high seed sensitivity (mean Ign-F1 43.58, std 3.80pp, range 39.74--47.34). Removing evidence entirely (no-evidence SFT, s42) reaches 47.21 Ign-F1.
14	
15	RSFT CED achieves 44.82 Ign-F1 (+1.24 over SFT mean) with substantially lower variance (std 0.51pp), though these reflect different sources of variation (Section~\ref{sec:scaling}). Even the weakest RSFT seed (44.23) exceeds the SFT mean. Evidence quality improves (Evi-F1 80.96 vs.\ 79.61) and coupling tightens (EDCR 0.6487 vs.\ 0.6797). Per-seed results appear in Appendix~\ref{sec:ablation_full}.
16

35	
36	
37	\subsection{Evidence-Extraction Decoupling Spectrum}
38	\label{sec:spectrum}
39	
40	Because evidence and extraction share gradient capacity, different optimization paradigms handle this competition in qualitatively different ways. We organize the outcomes along a \emph{decoupling spectrum} from catastrophic collapse to no decoupling (Figure~\ref{fig:spectrum}; full data in Table~\ref{tab:spectrum}, Appendix; training dynamics in Figure~\ref{fig:grpo_dynamics}, Appendix).
41	
42	\paragraph{GRPO.}
43	Under QLoRA 4-bit quantization ($G{=}4$), GRPO drives Rel-F1 to 1.38 while Evi-F1 rises to 85.04 (EDCR 0.9876): quantization-constrained precision limits the effective parameter space, so the reward signal preferentially optimizes the easier sub-task~\citep{gao2023scaling}, resembling the quantized-actor divergence of \citet{li2026qurl}. Full-precision bf16 ($G{=}8$) avoids catastrophe (Rel-F1 45.49, EDCR 0.6318), confirming quantization precision rather than group size as the primary factor.
44	
45	\paragraph{DPO and RSFT.}
46	DPO exhibits substantial extraction degradation: Rel-F1 drops from 33.37 (1 epoch) to 31.45 (3 epochs) while Evi-F1 rises (80.90$\to$84.84). EDCR is non-monotonic (0.8123$\to$0.7503): despite worsening extraction, evidence selectivity improves with continued training, partially reducing the decoupling. RSFT maintains both capabilities simultaneously (Rel-F1 45.80, Evi-F1 80.96, EDCR 0.6487) because discrete output selection cannot exploit the extraction-evidence trade-off.
47	
48	\begin{figure}[t]
49	\centering
50	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{fig_spectrum.pdf}
51	\caption{Decoupling spectrum. Each method is plotted by Rel-F1 (x-axis) and Evi-F1 (y-axis). Arrows indicate training progression for DPO and GRPO bf16. Decoupled methods drift toward the upper-left.}
52	\label{fig:spectrum}
53	\end{figure}
54	
55	
56	\subsection{Scale-Dependent Evidence Tax}
57	\label{sec:scaling}
58	
59	The evidence tax, defined as Ign-F1(no-evidence) $-$ Ign-F1(with-evidence) on the development set, is scale-dependent. Under matched evaluation conditions (\texttt{max\_new\_tokens}$=$2048, format-compliant intersection; Appendix~\ref{sec:training_tax}), the training-time tax reveals a within-family capacity threshold: evidence generation benefits 1.7B ($-$5.63pp; Appendix~\ref{sec:training_tax}), where it also halves the output duplication rate (60.5\%$\to$33.0\%; Table~\ref{tab:mechanistic} in Appendix~\ref{sec:fair_subset}), but is approximately neutral at $\geq$4B (+0.25$\pm$0.47pp at 4B, $n{=}3$; +0.04pp at 8B; +0.03pp at 14B). Table~\ref{tab:scaling} and Figure~\ref{fig:evidence_tax} present RSFT performance and inference-time evidence tax across five models (Figure~\ref{fig:scaling} in Appendix). Evidence training also affects SFT seed variance in a scale-dependent manner: at 4B and 8B, with-evidence models show lower cross-seed spread than no-evidence models (25$\times$ and 1.4$\times$ compression, respectively), but at 1.7B the pattern reverses---with-evidence variance exceeds no-evidence variance, likely amplified by extreme truncation rates at this scale (Appendix~\ref{sec:training_tax}).
60	
61	RSFT navigates this capacity landscape through quality-based data selection rather than gradient pressure. Mean Ign-F1 improves across tested scales: +6.55pp at 1.7B, +1.24pp at 4B, +4.14pp at 8B, and +3.44pp at 14B (Table~\ref{tab:scaling}). RSFT from an independent SFT base (s44, Method B) achieves 51.52 Ign-F1 at 4B (+4.18pp over that base). This result confirms that RSFT gains are not contingent on a specific SFT checkpoint.
62	
63	Beyond mean performance, RSFT consistently reduces training variance (sign test: 4/4 scales, $p{=}0.0625$; $\chi^2$ CI analysis confirms non-overlapping intervals at the 1.7B scale; Cohen's $d \geq 1.79$ for 3/4 scales; Appendix~\ref{sec:variance_ci}). Three RSFT seeds from a shared SFT checkpoint yield standard deviations of 0.09pp at 1.7B, 0.51pp at 4B, and 0.50pp at 8B ($n{=}3$ each), compared to SFT cross-seed variation of 2.79pp, 3.80pp, and 3.23pp respectively. While these measure different sources of variance (SFT reflects full training runs from different random seeds; RSFT reflects additional fine-tuning from a fixed base), the practical consequence is direct: across all Qwen3 scales with $\geq$3 seeds, variance compression is 32.6$\times$ at 1.7B, 7.5$\times$ at 4B, and 6.5$\times$ at 8B (point estimates from $N{=}3$ seeds; confidence intervals are wide, Appendix~\ref{sec:variance_ci}). Per-scale Rel-F1, Evi-F1, and EDCR breakdowns appear in Table~\ref{tab:cross_detail} (Appendix~\ref{sec:scaling_details}). The 1.7B RSFT model (51.19 Ign-F1, std 0.09pp) approaches 8B SFT performance (50.19, std 3.23pp) at 4.7$\times$ fewer parameters---comparable in mean yet 36$\times$ more predictable.
64	
65	Cross-family validation on LLaMA-3.1-8B confirms these patterns. All three RSFT seeds (55.87--57.14) exceed all three SFT seeds (48.02--53.84), yielding complete pairwise seed separation (Cohen's $d{=}2.58$) and 4.6$\times$ variance compression (std 2.92pp $\to$ 0.64pp). The SFT seed lottery is starkest at s44 (48.02 Ign-F1, 24.4\% truncation, elevated Evi-F1 85\%), a degenerate outcome that RSFT eliminates entirely (s44: 55.87). This robustness extends to inference-time format changes: LLaMA SFT models evaluated without evidence at 1024 tokens suffer 38--42\% truncation with Ign-F1 collapsing to ${\sim}$0.33 (s43/s44), while RSFT models truncate only 18--19\% with stable performance (55.41$\pm$0.13pp), indicating that CED filtering selects for compact output structure.
66	
67	For RSFT models, the inference-time evidence tax (same model evaluated with vs.\ without evidence output) preserves the benefit direction at 1.7B ($-$7.75pp, confirmed with 2048-token no-evidence evaluation that rules out truncation). At 4B, where the training-time tax is approximately neutral (+0.25$\pm$0.47pp; Appendix~\ref{sec:training_tax}), the RSFT inference-time tax is similarly small (+0.94pp). At 8B, the tax reverses to a benefit ($-$0.78pp), and at 14B a benefit is observed ($-$2.78$\pm$2.49pp, two-seed mean), directionally consistent with the 8B pattern though the CI crosses zero at $n{=}2$. RSFT thus maintains or improves extraction quality with evidence across the Qwen3 family. LLaMA-3.1-8B RSFT shows a larger inference-time evidence benefit ($-$2.17$\pm$0.76pp, three-seed mean, vs.\ $-$0.78pp for Qwen3-8B), confirming that both performance gains and training reliability generalize across model families.
68	
69	The effect of RSFT on inference-time evidence dependence is heterogeneous. At 1.7B, RSFT maintains strong evidence benefit ($-$5.01$\pm$0.59pp, two-seed mean), comparable to SFT ($-$4.86pp). At 4B, RSFT introduces a mild evidence cost (+1.65$\pm$0.29pp, three-seed mean), the Qwen3 scale with the largest positive RSFT test tax. Within the Qwen3 family at $\geq$8B, RSFT approaches evidence neutrality ($-$0.91$\pm$0.74pp at 8B, three-seed mean). Cross-architecture, LLaMA-3.1-8B shows partial compression: SFT tax of $-$4.94pp reduces to $-$1.46$\pm$0.40pp (two-seed mean) under RSFT, consistent with the cross-family variation observed in SFT evidence tax (Appendix~\ref{sec:training_tax}).
70

74	\label{tab:scaling}
75	\small

80	\midrule
81	Qwen3-1.7B & 44.64{\scriptsize$\pm$2.79}$^{(3)}$ & 51.19{\scriptsize$\pm$0.09}$^{(3)}$ & $-$7.75 & +6.55 \\

83	Qwen3-8B & 50.19{\scriptsize$\pm$3.23}$^{(3)}$ & 54.33{\scriptsize$\pm$0.50}$^{(3)}$ & $-$0.78 & +4.14 \\

85	LLaMA-3.1-8B & 51.09{\scriptsize$\pm$2.92}$^{(3)}$ & 56.54{\scriptsize$\pm$0.64}$^{(3)}$ & $-$2.17{\scriptsize$\pm$0.76}$^{(3)}$ & +5.45 \\

88	}

91	\end{table}
92	
93	\begin{figure}[t]
94	\centering
95	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{fig_evidence_tax.pdf}
96	\vspace{-2mm}
97	\caption{Inference-time evidence tax across model scales (RSFT models, Re-DocRED test). Negative values indicate evidence \emph{benefits} extraction. Error bars show $\pm$1 std (all scales have $\geq$2 seeds).}
98	\label{fig:evidence_tax}
99	\end{figure}
100	
101	\subsection{Evidence Strategy Comparison}
102	\label{sec:evidence_strategy}
103	
104	We compare joint generation against post-hoc BM25 retrieval (entity-constrained, $k{=}2$), using Evi-F1 (joint) with the DREEAM-protocol denominator~\citep{ma2023dreeam}. Post-hoc retrieval achieves higher Rel-F1 (48.76) by avoiding the evidence tax but lower Evi-F1 (joint) of 34.7 (EDCR 0.766). RSFT CED reaches 36.1 (EDCR 0.6487), a 1.4-point evidence advantage. CED reranking at inference (best-of-$N{=}8$ by CED score) achieves Rel-F1 48.64 and Evi-F1 81.88 (TP-only), approaching no-evidence extraction quality while preserving evidence.
105	
106	
107	\subsection{Generalization}
108	\label{sec:generalization}
109	
110	Cross-architecture validation on LLaMA-3.1-8B (Table~\ref{tab:scaling}) shows the largest RSFT gain (+5.45pp) with complete pairwise seed separation (\S\ref{sec:scaling}). The RSFT improvement transfers across datasets: on Re-DocRED~\citep{tan2022redocred}, RSFT achieves 41.45 Ign-F1 (+4.86pp over SFT 36.59), matching the DocRED gain (+5.08pp). Our best joint Evi-F1 (36.1, DREEAM-protocol) falls below supervised DREEAM (54.15\%~\citep{ma2023dreeam}), a gap driven by extraction performance rather than evidence quality (TP-only Evi-F1 exceeds 80\%). On the Re-DocRED test set, RSFT Ign-F1 drops approximately 20pp relative to dev across all evaluated scales (e.g., 14B: 58.5$\to$38.5), attributable to 2.82$\times$ higher annotation density in test (34.7 vs.\ 12.3 gold triples per document); model ranking is perfectly preserved (Spearman $\rho{=}1.0$; Table~\ref{tab:test_results} in Appendix). Full results appear in Appendix~\ref{sec:generalization_full}.
111	
112	
113	\subsection{Discussion}
114	\label{sec:analysis}
115	
116	EDCR (\S\ref{sec:edcr}) validates as a diagnostic at both cross-method ($\rho = -0.96$, $n = 7$ unique methods, $p < 0.001$; Appendix~\ref{sec:edcr_per_relation}) and per-relation levels ($\rho = -0.79$, $n = 76$, $p < 0.001$; Appendix~\ref{sec:edcr_per_relation}), the latter orthogonal to Evi-F1 ($\rho = -0.06$); partial correlations controlling for shared components are virtually unchanged ($\rho = -0.9617$ vs.\ $-0.9643$). CED and flat-NLI produce comparable RSFT results (Rel-F1 45.63 vs.\ 45.61); stricter selection ($k{=}1$) outperforms $k{=}3$ by 1.02 Ign-F1 despite fewer examples (Appendix~\ref{sec:ablation_full}). RSFT reduces truncation FN, hallucinated FP, and duplicates by 6--22\% across scales, but evidence quality is unchanged ($\Delta < 0.4$pp; Appendix~\ref{sec:error_analysis}): RSFT improves \emph{what} the model extracts but not \emph{how well} it grounds extractions---concrete evidence citation comparisons in Appendix~\ref{sec:qualitative} illustrate this pattern. Inference overhead analysis (Appendix~\ref{sec:inference_efficiency}) reveals that evidence generation adds 16--24\% wall-clock time at 4B--14B scale but paradoxically reduces inference time at 1.7B by 13.6\%, as the evidence field constrains output structure and reduces prediction count by 1.75$\times$.
117

Mean $\\pm$ std$^{(\\text{seeds})}$.}\n\\label{tab:scaling}\n\\small\n\\resizebox{\\columnwidth}{!}{%\n\\begin{tabular}{lcccc}\n\\toprule\n\\textbf{Model} & \\textbf{SFT w/ evi} & \\textbf{RSFT} & \\textbf{RSFT Tax}$^\\dagger$ & \\textbf{RSFT Gain} \\\\\n\\midrule\nQwen3-1.7B & 44.64{\\scriptsize$\\pm$2.79}$^{(3)}$ & 51.19{\\scriptsize$\\pm$0.09}$^{(3)}$ & $-$7.75 & +6.55 \\\\\nQwen3-4B & 43.58{\\scriptsize$\\pm$3.80}$^{(3)}$ & 44.82{\\scriptsize$\\pm$0.51}$^{(3)}$ & +0.94 & +1.24 \\\\\nQwen3-8B & 50.19{\\scriptsize$\\pm$3.23}$^{(3)}$ & 54.33{\\scriptsize$\\pm$0.50}$^{(3)}$ & $-$0.78 & +4.14 \\\\\nQwen3-14B & 55.07$^{(1)}$ & 58.51{\\scriptsize$\\pm$0.50}$^{(2)}$ & $-$2.78{\\scriptsize$\\pm$2.49}$^{(2)}$ & +3.44 \\\\\nLLaMA-3.1-8B & 51.09{\\scriptsize$\\pm$2.92}$^{(3)}$ & 56.54{\\scriptsize$\\pm$0.64}$^{(3)}$ & $-$2.17{\\scriptsize$\\pm$0.76}$^{(3)}$ & +5.45 \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}%\n}\n\\vspace{1mm}\n{\\raggedright\\footnotesize $^\\dagger$Inference-time: same RSFT model with/without evidence. No-evi uses @2048; with-evi uses @1024 (1.7B: @2048). Tax $=$ no-evi $-$ with-evi; positive $=$ cost. Training-time tax in Appendix~\\ref{sec:training_tax}.\\par}\n\\end{table}\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{fig_evidence_tax.pdf}\n\\vspace{-2mm}\n\\caption{Inference-time evidence tax across model scales (RSFT models, Re-DocRED test). Negative values indicate evidence \\emph{benefits} extraction. Error bars show $\\pm$1 std (all scales have $\\geq$2 seeds).}\n\\label{fig:evidence_tax}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\subsection{Evidence Strategy Comparison}\n\\label{sec:evidence_strategy}\n\nWe compare joint generation against post-hoc BM25 retrieval (entity-constrained, $k{=}2$), using Evi-F1 (joint) with the DREEAM-protocol denominator~\\citep{ma2023dreeam}. Post-hoc retrieval achieves higher Rel-F1 (48.76) by avoiding the evidence tax but lower Evi-F1 (joint) of 34.7 (EDCR 0.766). RSFT CED reaches 36.1 (EDCR 0.6487), a 1.4-point evidence advantage. CED reranking at inference (best-of-$N{=}8$ by CED score) achieves Rel-F1 48.64 and Evi-F1 81.88 (TP-only), approaching no-evidence extraction quality while preserving evidence.\n\n\n\\subsection{Generalization}\n\\label{sec:generalization}\n\nCross-architecture validation on LLaMA-3.1-8B (Table~\\ref{tab:scaling}) shows the largest RSFT gain (+5.45pp) with complete pairwise seed separation (\\S\\ref{sec:scaling}). The RSFT improvement transfers across datasets: on Re-DocRED~\\citep{tan2022redocred}, RSFT achieves 41.45 Ign-F1 (+4.86pp over SFT 36.59), matching the DocRED gain (+5.08pp). Our best joint Evi-F1 (36.1, DREEAM-protocol) falls below supervised DREEAM (54.15\\%~\\citep{ma2023dreeam}), a gap driven by extraction performance rather than evidence quality (TP-only Evi-F1 exceeds 80\\%). On the Re-DocRED test set, RSFT Ign-F1 drops approximately 20pp relative to dev across all evaluated scales (e.g., 14B: 58.5$\\to$38.5), attributable to 2.82$\\times$ higher annotation density in test (34.7 vs.\\ 12.3 gold triples per document); model ranking is perfectly preserved (Spearman $\\rho{=}1.0$; Table~\\ref{tab:test_results} in Appendix). Full results appear in Appendix~\\ref{sec:generalization_full}.\n\n\n\\subsection{Discussion}\n\\label{sec:analysis}\n\nEDCR (\\S\\ref{sec:edcr}) validates as a diagnostic at both cross-method ($\\rho = -0.96$, $n = 7$ unique methods, $p < 0.001$; Appendix~\\ref{sec:edcr_per_relation}) and per-relation levels ($\\rho = -0.79$, $n = 76$, $p < 0.001$; Appendix~\\ref{sec:edcr_per_relation}), the latter orthogonal to Evi-F1 ($\\rho = -0.06$); partial correlations controlling for shared components are virtually unchanged ($\\rho = -0.9617$ vs.\\ $-0.9643$). CED and flat-NLI produce comparable RSFT results (Rel-F1 45.63 vs.\\ 45.61); stricter selection ($k{=}1$) outperforms $k{=}3$ by 1.02 Ign-F1 despite fewer examples (Appendix~\\ref{sec:ablation_full}). RSFT reduces truncation FN, hallucinated FP, and duplicates by 6--22\\% across scales, but evidence quality is unchanged ($\\Delta < 0.4$pp; Appendix~\\ref{sec:error_analysis}): RSFT improves \\emph{what} the model extracts but not \\emph{how well} it grounds extractions---concrete evidence citation comparisons in Appendix~\\ref{sec:qualitative} illustrate this pattern. Inference overhead analysis (Appendix~\\ref{sec:inference_efficiency}) reveals that evidence generation adds 16--24\\% wall-clock time at 4B--14B scale but paradoxically reduces inference time at 1.7B by 13.6\\%, as the evidence field constrains output structure and reduces prediction count by 1.75$\\times$.\n",

1	\section{Training Hyperparameters}
2	\label{sec:hyperparams}
3

SFT and RSFT share the same optimizer settings; RSFT differs only in data construction (top-1 CED-selected outputs from $N{=}8$ candidates). GRPO and DPO use lower learning rates to stabilize post-SFT optimization.

39	\section{Full Ablation Results}
40	\label{sec:ablation_full}
41	
42	Table~\ref{tab:ablation_full} reports the complete RSFT ablation across scoring functions, top-$k$ values, and random seeds. RSFT CED $k{=}1$ achieves a four-seed mean Ign-F1 of 44.66 (standard deviation 0.53), confirming robust improvement over the SFT s42 baseline (39.74). The three-seed mean reported in Table~\ref{tab:main} (44.82, seeds 42/43/44) is slightly higher because it excludes the lowest seed (s45: 44.16). Random selection RSFT $k{=}1$ (41.14 Ign-F1), which samples from the same candidate pool without CED scoring, improves only +1.40pp over SFT s42 versus +4.92pp for CED RSFT, confirming that CED scoring precision drives the majority of the RSFT gain. Increasing $k$ to 3 slightly degrades performance (43.64 Ign-F1), consistent with the intuition that stricter selection yields higher-quality training data. The flat-NLI scoring variant produces comparable Rel-F1 (mean 45.61 vs.\ 45.63 for CED, a difference of 0.02pp). Both CED and flat-NLI improve substantially over the SFT baseline while maintaining or improving Evi-F1, confirming that the RSFT gains are driven by quality-based data selection rather than by the specific contrastive formulation.
43	
44	\paragraph{NLI scoring comparison.} Across all available seeds, CED RSFT achieves mean Rel-F1 of 45.63 (seeds 42, 43, 44, 45: range 45.14--46.21), and flat-NLI RSFT achieves mean Rel-F1 of 45.61 (seeds 42, 43: range 45.35--45.86). The 0.02pp difference suggests comparable performance: CED's contrastive design does not substantially outperform simpler NLI scoring for RSFT data selection. We adopt CED as the default because its contrastive formulation produces per-triple discrimination scores that support interpretable error analysis.
45	
46	\paragraph{Top-$k$ selection.} Best-of-8 ($k{=}1$) yields mean Ign-F1 44.66 and Rel-F1 45.63, while top-3-of-8 ($k{=}3$) produces 43.64 and 44.54. The selected training data confirms the quality gap: $k{=}1$ data has mean extraction F1 of 61.57 and mean CED of 0.6683, versus 57.61 and 0.6205 for $k{=}3$. Despite $k{=}3$ providing 2.96$\times$ more training examples, the additional lower-ranked samples introduce noise that degrades final performance.
47	
48	\paragraph{NLI scorer capacity.} CED filtering is robust to NLI model capacity: replacing the default scorer (DeBERTa-v3-base, 86M parameters) with DeBERTa-v3-large (304M) yields only +1.10pp Ign-F1 (45.33 vs.\ 44.23, s42), a difference within the v3-base seed noise of 0.88pp (s42: 44.23, s44: 45.11). Other metrics confirm the pattern: Rel-F1 improves by +1.19pp (46.36 vs.\ 45.17), Evi-F1 by +0.20pp (81.94 vs.\ 81.74), while EDCR shifts by $-$0.39pp (0.6530 vs.\ 0.6569)---the inconsistent direction across metrics rules out systematic improvement from a larger scorer. This insensitivity supports v3-base as a cost-effective default, as a 3.5$\times$ larger scorer does not materially improve CED-based data selection.

RSFT CED $k{=}1$ achieves a four-seed mean Ign-F1 of 44.66 (standard deviation 0.53), confirming robust improvement over the SFT s42 baseline (39.74). The three-seed mean reported in Table~\\ref{tab:main} (44.82, seeds 42/43/44) is slightly higher because it excludes the lowest seed (s45: 44.16). Random selection RSFT $k{=}1$ (41.14 Ign-F1), which samples from the same candidate pool without CED scoring, improves only +1.40pp over SFT s42 versus +4.92pp for CED RSFT, confirming that CED scoring precision drives the majority of the RSFT gain. Increasing $k$ to 3 slightly degrades performance (43.64 Ign-F1), consistent with the intuition that stricter selection yields higher-quality training data. The flat-NLI scoring variant produces comparable Rel-F1 (mean 45.61 vs.\\ 45.63 for CED, a difference of 0.02pp). Both CED and flat-NLI improve substantially over the SFT baseline while maintaining or improving Evi-F1, confirming that the RSFT gains are driven by quality-based data selection rather than by the specific contrastive formulation.\n\n\\paragraph{NLI scoring comparison.} Across all available seeds, CED RSFT achieves mean Rel-F1 of 45.63 (seeds 42, 43, 44, 45: range 45.14--46.21), and flat-NLI RSFT achieves mean Rel-F1 of 45.61 (seeds 42, 43: range 45.35--45.86). The 0.02pp difference suggests comparable performance: CED's contrastive design does not substantially outperform simpler NLI scoring for RSFT data selection. We adopt CED as the default because its contrastive formulation produces per-triple discrimination scores that support interpretable error analysis.\n\n\\paragraph{Top-$k$ selection.} Best-of-8 ($k{=}1$) yields mean Ign-F1 44.66 and Rel-F1 45.63, while top-3-of-8 ($k{=}3$) produces 43.64 and 44.54. The selected training data confirms the quality gap: $k{=}1$ data has mean extraction F1 of 61.57 and mean CED of 0.6683, versus 57.61 and 0.6205 for $k{=}3$. Despite $k{=}3$ providing 2.96$\\times$ more training examples, the additional lower-ranked samples introduce noise that degrades final performance.\n\n\\paragraph{NLI scorer capacity.} CED filtering is robust to NLI model capacity: replacing the default scorer (DeBERTa-v3-base, 86M parameters) with DeBERTa-v3-large (304M) yields only +1.10pp Ign-F1 (45.33 vs.\\ 44.23, s42), a difference within the v3-base seed noise of 0.88pp (s42: 44.23, s44: 45.11). Other metrics confirm the pattern: Rel-F1 improves by +1.19pp (46.36 vs.\\ 45.17), Evi-F1 by +0.20pp (81.94 vs.\\ 81.74), while EDCR shifts by $-$0.39pp (0.6530 vs.\\ 0.6569)---the inconsistent direction across metrics rules out systematic improvement from a larger scorer. This insensitivity supports v3-base as a cost-effective default, as a 3.5$\\times$ larger scorer does not materially improve CED-based data selection.\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\caption{Full RSFT ablation on DocRED dev set (Qwen3-4B). 117	\section{Variance Compression Confidence Intervals}
118	\label{sec:variance_ci}
119	
120	Table~\ref{tab:variance_ci} reports 95\% confidence intervals for SFT and RSFT standard deviations using the $\chi^2$ distribution ($N{=}3$ seeds per configuration). At 1.7B, the confidence intervals are fully non-overlapping (SFT lower bound 1.45pp $>$ RSFT upper bound 0.54pp), providing the strongest evidence for variance compression. At the remaining three scales, intervals overlap but point estimates are consistently in the same direction (SFT std $>$ RSFT std at all four scales; sign test $p = 0.0625$, one-sided binomial, $n = 4$, underpowered). Cohen's $d$ ranges from 0.46 (4B) to 3.31 (1.7B), with 1.7B and LLaMA-8B showing large effect sizes ($d > 2.5$).

144	\section{Controlled Evaluation Subset}
145	\label{sec:fair_subset}
146	
147	The 1.7B evidence tax comparison (\S\ref{sec:scaling}) is confounded by differential truncation rates. At \texttt{max\_new\_tokens}$=$1024, the no-evidence model truncates 50.9\% of outputs (501/985 documents) versus 31.9\% (314/985) for the evidence model. To address this, we adopt a matched@2048 fair evaluation: both configurations are evaluated at \texttt{max\_new\_tokens}$=$2048, and the tax is computed on the intersection of documents where both produce format-compliant output. This yields 971/985 fair-subset documents, with a tax of $-$5.63pp (s42), confirming a genuine 1.7B evidence benefit. As additional validation, a stricter controlled subset at @1024 (excluding truncated or repetitive outputs; 442/985 documents) yields $-$6.2pp, with the stronger benefit attributable to the more aggressive filtering.
148	
149	Table~\ref{tab:mechanistic} provides a mechanistic breakdown across three scales (evaluated at @1024). Output duplication rate (fraction of documents containing $\geq$3 repeated triples or unique/total $< 0.5$) declines monotonically with scale for both training configurations, but the gap between no-evidence and with-evidence models narrows from 27.5pp at 1.7B to 3.7pp at 8B. This pattern supports the implicit regularization hypothesis: evidence generation forces smaller models to attend to supporting context, reducing degenerate repetition. At 4B, duplication is already moderate (31.1\% with evidence), so the regularization benefit diminishes; under matched@2048 evaluation, the 4B training-time tax is approximately neutral (+0.25$\pm$0.47pp, three-seed mean). The truncation asymmetry reverses across scales: at 1.7B the no-evidence model truncates 19pp more often; at 8B the evidence model truncates 9pp more often due to the longer output format.

170	\section{Training-Time Evidence Tax}
171	\label{sec:training_tax}
172	
173	The evidence tax can be measured in two ways. The \emph{training-time} tax compares separately trained models (SFT with evidence vs.\ SFT without evidence in the training output format). The \emph{inference-time} tax (reported in Table~\ref{tab:scaling}) evaluates the same RSFT model with and without evidence at inference time.
174	
175	Na\"ive comparisons at \texttt{max\_new\_tokens}$=$1024 are confounded by differential truncation rates (e.g., 50.9\% vs.\ 31.9\% at 1.7B). To control for this, we adopt a \emph{matched@2048 fair evaluation}: both configurations are evaluated at \texttt{max\_new\_tokens}$=$2048, and the tax is computed on the intersection of documents where both models produce format-compliant output. Table~\ref{tab:training_tax} reports the matched@2048 training-time tax across Qwen3 scales.
176	
177	The tax reveals a within-family capacity threshold: evidence generation benefits 1.7B ($-$5.63pp on 971/985 fair-subset documents) but is approximately neutral at $\geq$4B (+0.25$\pm$0.47pp at 4B across three seeds, +0.04pp at 8B, +0.03pp at 14B). At 1.7B, s42 is the strongest SFT seed (47.86 Ign-F1, +3.22pp above the three-seed mean of 44.64), so the benefit may be amplified relative to the population mean. LLaMA-3.1-8B SFT training-time tax is consistently negative but with high magnitude variance ($-$1.53 to $-$11.20pp, mean $-$5.19$\pm$5.25pp under matched@2048) and omitted from the table due to the s43 outlier (16.3\% no-evidence format failure inflates its tax); cross-architecture patterns are addressed in \S\ref{sec:generalization_full}.
